|章节|行号范围|引用类型|概念名称|引用原文摘要|卫材相关性|
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
|Ch1|p.1|定义|Drying operation|Drying commonly describes the process of thermally removing volatile substances (moisture) to yield a solid product.|★★★★★｜纸尿裤芯体/绒屑浆干燥本质即热脱挥发分得到固体，定义全行业操作|
|Ch1|p.1|数据|Dryer diversity & energy|Over 400 types of dryers have been reported whereas over 100 distinct types are commonly available. … Energy consumption in drying ranges from a low value of under 5% for the chemical process industries to 35% for the papermaking operations.|★★★★★｜绒屑浆/卫生纸干燥占造纸能耗高达35%，是成本与选型首要约束|
|Ch1|p.1|定义|Bound vs unbound moisture|Moisture held in loose chemical combination … which exerts a vapor pressure less than that of pure liquid, is called bound moisture. Moisture in excess of bound moisture is called unbound moisture.|★★★★★｜SAP与绒屑中"结合水"决定残水下限与干燥终点，是残湿规格的物理基础|
|Ch1|p.2|数据|Drying time & speed range|Drying times range from 0.25 s (drying of tissue paper) to 5 months (for certain hardwood species) … Product speeds range from 0 (stationary) to 2000 m/min (tissue paper)|★★★★★｜卫生纸机2000 m/min、0.25s干燥，对照绒毛浆/无纺布干燥时间尺度选型|
|Ch1|p.3|论断|Convective dominance|Over 85% of industrial dryers are of the convective type with hot air or direct combustion gases as the drying medium. Over 99% of the applications involve removal of water.|★★★★★｜热风对流是绒屑/无纺布穿透干燥主流，排气焓损失大，催生节能改造|
|Ch1|p.3|论断|Scale-up difficulty|all processes involved, even in the simplest dryer, are highly nonlinear and hence scale-up of dryers is generally very difficult.|★★★★☆｜混合芯体(绒屑+SAP+胶)放大无通用准则，须中试|
|Ch1|p.12|定义|Solid classification|Hygroscopic-porous media, such as clay, molecular sieves, wood, and textiles. … Colloidal (nonporous) media, such as soap, glue, some polymers (e.g., nylons)|★★★★★｜绒屑属毛细多孔、SAP/尼龙属高吸湿/胶体介质，决定内部传质机理|
|Ch1|p.12|定义|Equilibrium moisture content|the moisture content of the solid is called the equilibrium moisture content under the prevailing conditions.|★★★★★｜仓库RH/T下的平衡含水率X*即残湿规格下限|
|Ch1|p.13|图表|Sorption isotherm shape|Figure 1.7 Shapes of sorption isotherms for materials of varying hygroscopicity. … PVC powder (50°C) … mildly hygroscopic|★★★★☆｜PVC/PE膜与SAP的吸湿等温线形态决定防潮包装与残湿窗口|
|Ch1|p.14|公式|Temp coefficient of X*|@ X* / @ T = -A X* ; The coefficient A lies between 0.005 and 0.01 per kelvin for … natural and synthetic fibers, wood, and potatoes.|★★★★★｜纤维/木材的A=0.005–0.01 K⁻¹可直接估绒屑在储存温度变化下的残湿漂移|
|Ch1|p.14|论断|Sorption-desorption hysteresis|The equilibrium moisture content of a product may be different depending on whether the product is wetted (sorption) or dried (desorption)|★★★｜芯体再吸湿滞后影响仓储与开包后手感，需按解吸支设定|
|Ch1|p.16|论断|Falling-rate control|In the constant rate period the rate of drying is determined by the rate of evaporation. When all the exposed surface of the solid ceases to be wetted, vapor movement by diffusion and capillarity from within the solid to the surface are the rate-controlling steps.|★★★★★｜厚铺芯体干燥落在降速段，传质控制而非传热，决定停留时间|
|Ch1|p.16|论断|Thickness-squared scaling|the rate of drying during the falling rate period varies as the square of the material thickness, which indicates the desirability of granulating the feedstock … or using thin layers|★★★★★｜芯体厚度越大干燥越慢(平方关系)，指导薄层/打散投料|
|Ch1|p.17|定义|Characteristic drying curve|all the curves could often be approximated to a single curve, 'characteristic' of a particular substance. … widely used and often utilized for interpolation and prediction of dryer performance|★★★｜归一化干燥曲线可作中试插值工具，但判据严格、适用范围有限|
|Ch1|p.18|论断|Overdrying penalty|Excessive drying is wasteful; … often overdrying results in a degraded product, as in the case of paper and timber.|★★★★☆｜过度干燥使纸/绒屑降级并增能耗，须设上限|
|Ch1|p.19|案例|Paper drying demand|The largest demand for drying equipment is for the continuous drying of paper, which is done on cylinder or 'can' dryers.|★★★★★｜卫生纸/绒屑浆连续干燥主设备为缸(罐)式干燥机，直接对应产线|
|Ch1|p.20|论断|Wet-bulb in constant rate|In the initial constant rate drying period … the solid surface takes on the wet bulb temperature corresponding to the air temperature and humidity conditions|★★★★☆｜恒速段表面温度=湿球温度，限定热敏纤维表面最高温|
|Ch1|p.21|定义|Conduction/indirect dryer|Conduction or indirect dryers are more appropriate for thin products or for very wet solids. Heat for evaporation is supplied through heated surfaces|★★★★☆｜间接干燥热效率更高，适合薄型非织造/湿浆，对应Ch6|
|Ch1|p.21|论断|Steam-environment sensitivity|For solids susceptible to oxidation or denaturation … the heat sensitivity can be quite different in a steam environment.|★★★★★｜SAP/纤维在过热蒸汽中热敏性不同，可选蒸汽干燥抗氧化黄变|
|Ch1|p.22|图表|Cylinder dryers for sheet|Sheet-form materials, supported on rolls — Cylinder dryers — Paper, textiles, pulp|★★★★★｜辊缸干燥机处理纸/纺织/浆板，正是绒屑浆板与面层无纺布设备|
|Ch1|p.23|论断|Energy/safety conflict|Explosion vents could be a hygiene problem. Dust in recycling streams fouls heat exchanger surfaces … High product collection efficiency … means high pressure drop|★★★★☆｜绒屑粉尘爆炸与回收效率/压降冲突，影响安全与能耗|
|Ch1|p.24|数据|Energy consumption table|Spray dryer 4,500–11,500 kJ/kg H2O evaporated; Drum dryer 3,200–6,500; Fluid bed 4,000–6,000; Rotary 4,600–9,200|★★★★☆｜各干燥机比能耗为吨产品能耗核算基线|
|Ch1|p.26|定义|GMP & hygienic design|Drying of food and biotechnological products require adherence to good manufacturing practice (GMP) and hygienic equipment design and operation.|★★★★★｜湿巾/医用无纺布属生物/食品级，干燥须GMP与卫生设计|
|Ch1|p.26|定义|Heat sensitivity checklist|Heat sensitivity — Melting point … Glass transition temperature … Sorption isotherms (equilibrium moisture content)|★★★★★｜玻璃化转变温度列入热敏清单，约束SAP/聚合物颗粒干燥温度上限|
|Ch1|p.27|论断|Superheated steam advantage|Superheated steam as the convective drying medium offers several advantages, e.g., higher drying rates … lower net energy consumption … elimination of fire and explosion hazard.|★★★★★｜过热蒸汽干燥可灭燃爆风险并节能，适合易燃绒屑/溶剂涂布湿巾|
|Ch1|p.28|论断|Select system not dryer|It is typically not a desirable option to depend exclusively on prior experience … Each drying problem deserves its own independent evaluation and solution.|★★★★★｜新卫材干燥问题须独立评估，不能照搬他线选型|
|Ch2|p.31|论断|参数必须实验测定|The calculation of drying processes requires a knowledge of... characteristics... cannot be calculated by analysis... can be determined only by adequate experiments.|★★★★★｜卫材新料(新SAP/新浆)干燥参数须实测，不可套文献值|
|Ch2|p.32|列表|干燥实验五大目标|1. Choice of adequate drying equipment 2. Establishing data required for planning 3. Investigation of efficiency and capacity 4. Effect of operational conditions on shape and quality 5. Study of mechanism|★★★★★｜新卫材线选型与质量(非织布完整性/SAP完整)由目标1/4直接约束|
|Ch2|p.32|定义|直接法测湿|determination of moisture content of a sample by drying in a drying oven... vacuum desiccator. material disintegrated into pieces of 1–2 mm3... 4–5 g... dried at 102–105°C... difference does not exceed ±0.05%.|★★★★★｜绒屑/SAP/无纺布来料残湿认证的参考标准方法|
|Ch2|p.32|数据|快速法偏差|results obtained at 130–150°C may deviate by 0.5–1.0%. Thus the quick method appears suitable only for an approximated determination|★★★★☆｜产线快速测湿仅为近似值，须以基准法校准|
|Ch2|p.33|定义|间接电法测湿|moisture determination based on the change of the ohmic dc resistance, a measurement of the electrostatic capacitance (dielectric constant), and a measurement of the loss in an ac field|★★★★★｜在线介电/电容测湿是非织布/浆线实时控制的唯一可行法|
|Ch2|p.33|定义|Karl-Fischer/蒸馏/萃取|Karl–Fischer analysis... distillation method with toluene... extraction method with absolute ethanol|★★★★★｜SAP为水溶胀网络，烤箱法会降解交联，宜用Karl-Fischer水特异性测定|
|Ch2|p.33|论断|气体湿含量测定|determination of absolute value by gravimetry or barometry (direct); dry and wet bulb temperatures and dew point (indirect); property such as absorptivity to electromagnetic waves|★★★★☆｜干燥排气湿含量监测(排潮/热回收)依赖间接法|

|Ch4|p.79|论断|数据稀缺|Nowadays, more and more sophisticated drying models are becoming available, but a major question that still remains is the measurement or determination of the parameters used in the models.|★★★★★｜SAP/蓬松浆复合材料的传热传质参数在文献中几乎空白，必须自建测量体系]|
|Ch4|p.81|定义|有效湿扩散系数|@X/@t = D ∇²X (4.1) where D (m²/s) is the effective diffusivity, X (kg/kg db) is the material moisture content, and t (s) is the time.|★★★★★｜蓬松浆网、SAP颗粒内部水分迁移的统一控制方程，所有干燥模拟的入口]|
|Ch4|p.81|论断|控制阻力决定精度需求|If drying is controlled by the internal moisture diffusion, then the effective moisture diffusivity must be known with high accuracy... Drying of small particles with low velocity of air is controlled by the external mass transfer.|★★★★★｜穿透干燥大颗粒床层可能由内部传热控制，薄网则由外部传质控制，需测的属性随几何翻转]|
|Ch4|p.82|文献|扩散方法源于聚合物|The diffusivity in solids can be determined using the methods presented in Table 4.1. These methods have been developed primarily for polymeric materials [7–9].|★★★★★｜Crank & Park《Diffusion in Polymers》正是SAP/非织造布水分扩散测量的方法源头]|
|Ch4|p.84|数据|聚合物扩散系数汇编|Effective diffusion coefficients are available for inorganic materials[20], polymers [8], and foods[21,22]... Cellulose acetate 2.0E-12–3.2E-12 m²/s at 25°C; Avicel 5.0E-9–5.0E-8.|★★★★★｜纤维素醋酸酯、微晶纤维素、淀粉凝胶即绒毛浆/无纺布/SAP载体化学，可直接作为首估量级]|
|Ch4|p.85|公式|Arrhenius扩散|D = D₀ exp(−E/RT) (4.2) where D₀ (m²/s) is the Arrhenius factor, E (kJ/kmol) is the activation energy for diffusion.|★★★★★｜SAP/蓬松浆的D随温度的Arrhenius关系，是干燥动力学温度窗口设定的基础]|
|Ch4|p.85|论断|聚合物反常扩散|for several of the less hydrophilic polymers (e.g., polymethacrylates and polyacrylates) the moisture diffusivity decreases with increasing water content... independent of the concentration for some hydrophobic polyolefins.|★★★★★｜聚丙烯酸酯即SAP主链化学，D随含水上升反而下降是溶胀/非Fickian干燥的指纹，必须建模为Case-II]|
|Ch4|p.86|公式|湿度-温度耦合扩散|Table 4.3 T3.3 Polyvinyl alcohol D(X,T) = a₀ exp(ΣaᵢXⁱ) exp(−a₂/T); T3.1 Apple/carrot/starch D = a₀ exp(a₁X) exp(−a₂/T).|★★★★★｜聚乙烯醇等聚合物的D(X,T)经验式可直接套用于非织造粘合剂与SAP的回归拟合]|
|Ch4|p.87|公式|孔隙模型|D = (ε/τ²) δ Dₐ (4.3) where ε is porosity, τ tortuosity, δ constrictivity, Dₐ vapor diffusivity in air.|★★★★★｜蓬松浆垫的D∝ε/τ²，孔隙率与曲折度是网结构设计的直接杠杆]|
|Ch4|p.88|定义|有效热导率|@T/@t = (k/(ρ cₚ)) ∇²T (4.4)... Equation 4.4 is used in cases in which heat transfer during drying takes place through conduction (internally controlled drying).|★★★★★｜厚蓬松垫内部导热控制时（Bi>1）用Fourier定律，决定热风穿透干燥的能量平衡]|
|Ch4|p.89|论断|Biot数判定|the drying process is internally controlled whenever the respective Biot number (Biₕ, Biₘ) is greater than 1[5].|★★★★★｜Bi>1内部控制调材料（基重/孔隙），Bi<0.1外部控制调空气，决定干燥机设计杠杆]|
|Ch4|p.91|数据|热导率汇编|Table 4.6: Paper 0.130 W/(m K); Bakelite 0.232; Rubber hard 0.150; Asbestos 0.225; Corkboard 0.043.|★★★★★｜纸、橡胶、酚醛（Bakelite）等聚合物/纤维素材料热导率可直接用于非织造布与芯体导热建模]|
|Ch4|p.92|公式|结构模型|Table 4.7 Parallel k = (1−ε)k₁ + εk₂; Perpendicular 1/k = (1−ε)/k₁ + ε/k₂; Maxwell model.|★★★★★｜异相纤维垫的热导率在并联（上限）与串联（下限）之间，可用于蓬松垫k的上下界预估]|
|Ch4|p.94|定义|边界换热系数|Q = hₕ A (Tₐ − Tₐ) (4.5)... J = hₘ A (Xₐₛ − Xₐ) (4.6)... used when Biot number (Biₕ, Biₘ) is less than 0.1.|★★★★★｜外部控制干燥（薄网/高速风）用Newton定律，hₘ是首要未知量]|
|Ch4|p.95|数据|h/hₘ经验关联|Table 4.9: T9.1 Packed beds jₕ = 1.06Re⁻⁰·⁴¹; T9.11 Fluidized Nu = 0.0133Re¹·⁶; T9.13 Spray Nu = 2 + 0.6Re¹/²Pr¹/³.|★★★★★｜流化床/喷动床/喷雾干燥器的h与hₘ关联式，SAP造粒与浆料喷涂干燥可直接取用]|
|Ch4|p.97|公式|Lewis关系|hₘ/h = 1/cₚ (4.13) where cₚ is specific heat of air.|★★★★★｜仅知传热关联时由h推hₘ，是非织造穿透干燥机首版尺寸估算的关键桥梁]|
|Ch4|p.98|公式|干燥常数|dX/dt = −K(X − Xₑ) (4.14)... The drying constant K is the most suitable quantity for purposes of design, optimization.|★★★★★｜薄层干燥常数K是快速首版干燥机尺寸与优化迭代的最简代理量]|
|Ch4|p.99|公式|K与D换算|K = π²D/L² (4.15) where D is the effective diffusivity and L the thickness of the slab.|★★★★★｜已知网厚L与干燥常数K即可反推D，或反过来用D预估值驱动K，闭环设计]|
|Ch4|p.100|定义|平衡含水率|The moisture content of the material when it comes into equilibrium with drying air... sorption isotherm.|★★★★★｜芯体复合材料在干燥空气中的终态含水由平衡等温线决定，是干燥终点与包装条件依据]|
|Ch4|p.100|论断|解吸等温线|It is clear from Figure 4.11 that the desorption isotherm is the curve to use for the process of drying.|★★★★★｜SAP颗粒在干燥机中失水必须用解吸支而非吸附支，避免高估干燥终点含水]|
|Ch4|p.100|论断|等温线类型|The sorption isotherms of the hydrophilic polymers, such as natural fibers and foods, are of type II. The isotherms of the less hydrophilic rubbers, plastics, synthetic fibers are of type III.|★★★★★｜纤维素绒毛≈II型强结合，PP/PE无纺布≈III型弱结合，决定芯体吸湿与干燥行为差异]|
|Ch4|p.102|公式|GAB模型|The Guggenheim–Anderson–de Boer (GAB) equation is considered as the most versatile model... over a wide range of water activities (0.1 < a_w < 0.9).|★★★★★｜GAB是设计干燥过程与包装的推荐等温式，应拟合蓬松浆+SAP混合物的Xₑ(a_w,T)]|
|Ch4|p.103|论断|同步估计|Theoretically, all the properties describing the drying kinetics could be estimated simultaneously... from a drying experimental apparatus.|★★★★★｜单次干燥实验回归得到D/k/h/hₘ，是卫材实验室自建物性数据库的标准方法]|
|Ch4|p.104|公式|干燥模型PDE组|Moisture diffusion @((−X)/@t) = ∇·(D∇X) (4.16); Boundary vapor ((−D∇X)·n) = hₘ(a_w − a_we) (4.18); Heat conduction @((−hₛ)/@t) = ∇·(k∇T) (4.20); Boundary heat (k∇T)·n = hₕ(Tₐ−Tₛ)−DH hₘ(a_w−a_we) (4.22).|★★★★★｜四方程耦合的工艺模型是蓬松浆/SAP干燥数字孪生的核心骨架]|
|Ch4|p.107|公式|食品扩散通用模型|D = D₀ exp(−E₀/RTᵣ)[1 + (X/X₀) exp(−Eᵢ/R(1/T − 1/Tᵣ))] (4.28); D₀=2.94E-7 m²/s exp(−1.58E3 K/T) exp(−6.72E-2 kg/kg/X).|★★★★★｜Arrhenius×湿度分裂为E₀(干)/Eᵢ(湿)的模板可照搬到纤维素/淀粉类卫材的D拟合]|
|Ch4|p.109|数据|模型食品参数|Table 4.16 Amioca D₀=1.52E-8, Dᵢ=1.52E-8, E₀=0, Eᵢ=33.3; Hylon-7 D₀=1.96E-8, Eᵢ=24.2; Potato D₀=1.57E-9, Eᵢ=76.9 kJ/mol.|★★★★★｜高直链/蜡质淀粉（SAP载体、胶凝剂）的D与活化能量级，可作为淀粉基卫材首估值]|
|Ch5|p.119|论断|电子表格成为工程标配|Spreadsheet software has become an indispensable tool for engineers, because of the availability of personal computers, ease of use, and adaptability to many types of problems.|★★★★★｜卫材工艺工程师可用Excel速建干燥机首版尺寸与成本模型，无需昂贵商业软件]|
|Ch5|p.119|论断|Excel+VBA有效|Microsoft Excel with Visual Basic for Applications is an effective tool for process design. Spreadsheets offer sufficient process model 'hospitality.'|★★★★★｜非织造穿透干燥/传送带干燥可用Excel+VBA搭建可交互模拟器]|
|Ch5|p.120|定义|建模/仿真/设计|Modeling: is the procedure to translate the physical laws of a process to mathematical equations; Simulation: predicts the real performance of a process; Design: is a procedure to size and rate a process.|★★★★★｜界定绒毛浆/芯体干燥项目的建模-仿真-设计三步边界，避免范围蔓延]|
|Ch5|p.121|公式|自由度分析|F = M – N (Degrees of freedom); Design variables D = F – K.|★★★★★｜设计变量数=自由度-规格数，限定蓬松垫干燥机最多可调4个旋钮（如风温/湿度/风速/带宽）]|
|Ch5|p.122|定义|模拟器四单元架构|Four different units are distinguished... 'Process Model Worksheet' is the heart of the system calculations. It contains the process model.|★★★★★｜工艺模型/数据库/问题求解/VBA/GUI四表分离，便于卫材实验室维护与替换物性]|
|Ch5|p.122|公式|六步实现法|Step 1 Workbook Preparation; Step 2 Process Modeling; Step 3 Using 'Solver' for Optimization; Step 4 Tables/Charts; Step 5 Dialog Boxes; Step 6 Integrated Graphics Interface.|★★★★★｜可直接照做的传送带干燥机电子表格搭建流程，适用于非织造布烘干线]|
|Ch5|p.123|公式|Solver优化骨架|Sub optimum()... SolverOk SetCell: Range('objective'), MaxMinVal: 1, ByChange: Range('variables')... constraints greater than zero.|★★★★★｜将TAC设为objective、带宽风参为variables、物理限值作constraints，一键优化干燥机]|
|Ch5|p.125|公式|干燥模型方程链|Equation T10.3 calculates the equilibrium material moisture content... T10.4 estimates the drying time constant... T10.5 calculates the required drying time.|★★★★★｜平衡含水率→干燥时间常数→所需干燥时间，是芯体复合材料干燥时间预测主线]|
|Ch5|p.126|公式|干燥时间积分式|t = t_c ln[(X₀ − Xₑ)/(X − Xₑ)] (T10.5)|★★★★★｜即Ch.4 Lewis薄层方程的积分形式，t_c=1/K可直接由薄层实验得到，闭环设计]|
|Ch5|p.126|公式|物料/空气衡算|W = F(X₀ − X) (T10.6); W = Fₐ(Y − Y₀) (T10.7)|★★★★★｜固体与空气两侧水分衡算，是穿透干燥机蒸发量与排风量计算基础]|
|Ch5|p.126|公式|热能需求|Q_we = F(X₀ − X)[ΔH₀ − (C_PL − C_PV)T] (T10.8); Q = Q_we + Q_sh + Q_ah (T10.11)|★★★★★｜水蒸发/固体加热/空气加热三项热能，决定加热器面积与能耗，用于卫材干燥能耗核算]|
|Ch5|p.126|公式|带几何与风速|A_b = L·D (T10.16); u_b = L/t (T10.17)|★★★★★｜带面积=长×宽、带速=长/时间，直接给出非织造网在传送带上的几何与速度]|
|Ch5|p.126|公式|风机压降与功率|ΔP = f₁·Z₀·V² (T10.18); Fᵢ = ρₐ·V·D·L (T10.19); E_f = ΔP·F/r (T10.20)|★★★★★｜穿透干燥机的风阻与风机功率估算，是卫材干燥线公用工程负荷依据]|
|Ch5|p.127|数据|问题规模|Process variables 37 / Degrees of freedom 13 / Specifications 9 / Design variables 4.|★★★★★｜示范干燥机37变量24方程、9规格4设计变量，卫材项目可照搬此自由度结构]|
|Ch5|p.127|定义|设计变量集|Design Variables: Y drying-air humidity, T drying-air temperature, V drying-air velocity, D belt width.|★★★★★｜传送带干燥机四可调变量即风参与带宽，卫材首版优化应锁定这四个]|
|Ch5|p.128|公式|总成本目标|Equipment cost C_eq = C_bel·A_bⁿ_bel + C_exc·A_sⁿ_exc + C_fan·E_fⁿ_fan (T17.1); TAC = e·C_eq + C_op (T17.3).|★★★★★｜年化总成本TAC作优化目标，使卫材干燥机在设备与运行成本间取得平衡]|
|Ch5|p.129|数据|数值算例|Feed 0.1 ton/h db, X₀=10, X=0.1 kg/kg db, d=0.01 m, T=90°C, V=1.5 m/s, D=2 m → L=12.6 m, A_b=25.3 m², Q=0.91 MW, TAC=357 k$/y.|★★★★★｜完整可复算算例，卫材工程师可照此替换物性得到自己产线的尺寸与TAC]|
|Ch5|p.131|数据|性能与成本|thermal efficiency η = 0.69; specific evaporation r = 39.1 kg/h·m²; Equipment 599 k$, Operating 207 k$/y.|★★★★★｜干燥强度与热效率量级为卫材干燥线基准，可用于新产线目标设定]|
|Ch5|p.131|论断|GUI现代化警告|DialogSheets(dbName)... (Table 5.9) relies on pre-2007 Excel dialog sheets.|★★★★★｜原文VBA用已废弃DialogSheets，卫材IT重建须改为UserForm否则GUI无法运行]|
|Ch6|p.135|定义|间接/接触/传导干燥|Indirect (or contact or conductive) dryers... are dryers in which the heating medium does not come into contact with the product being dried. Instead, wet material is dried by contact with a heated surface; heat transfer to the wet material is mainly by conduction from this surface.|★★★★★｜SAP颗粒、聚合物薄片、热敏非织造粘合剂适合在隔离加热面的接触干燥机中闭式干燥]|
|Ch6|p.135|论断|表面温度范围|The temperature of heat transfer surfaces may range from 40°C (as in freeze drying) to about 300°C.|★★★★★｜接触干燥壁温40–300°C，设定SAP/PP-PE粘合剂壁温须低于其Tg，避免软化粘连]|
|Ch6|p.135|论断|能效更高|Generally, indirect dryers have higher energy efficiency than the direct dryers, because the energy lost through the exhaust gas stream is greatly reduced.|★★★★★｜接触干燥排气损失小，适合卫材产线节能与低排放改造论证]|
|Ch6|p.136|数据|能耗对比|typical energy consumption is in the range of 2800–3600 kJ/kg of water evaporated compared to direct dryers, which consume 4000–6000 kJ/kg of water evaporated.|★★★★★｜接触干燥比对流干燥节能约30%，SAP/薄片干燥线opex差异显著]|
|Ch6|p.136|论断|高资本成本|indirect dryers have typically higher capital costs than equivalent direct dryers due to the need for heat exchange surfaces.|★★★★★｜接触干燥需换热面故capex更高，SAP干燥设备投资评估须计入]|
|Ch6|p.136|论断|卫生低排放优势|Indirect dryers also need minimal cleaning of the exhaust gas... very low emission of fines and particles... ideal for heat-sensitive materials such as foods, pharmaceuticals, and other biomaterials.|★★★★★｜闭式低尘低排适合卫材GMP/洁净要求及热敏聚合物干燥]|
|Ch6|p.137|定义|高温短时原则|An indirect dryer that uses high heat-source temperature should be operated in such a way that the residence time of the drying material within it is minimized.|★★★★★｜高壁温接触干燥须缩短停留，避免SAP/粘合剂在高温区过度受热]|
|Ch6|p.138|数据|托盘装载|each tray may be loaded up to a depth of about 40 mm giving a loading of approximately 40 kg of wet material/m² of tray area.|★★★★★｜托盘干燥浅层装载量为卫材小试/批次干燥实验设计参考]|

Hygroscopic materials may also be dried completely at temperatures lower than the maximum permissible temperatures.|★★★★★｜真空托盘/双锥适合热敏易氧化卫材聚合物，低温下可彻底干燥吸湿料]|
|Ch6|p.140|数据|蒸汽管回转干燥|The length to diameter ratio of the rotating shell can vary from 4:1 to 10:1... steam pressure 4–10 bar... thermal efficiency 70–90%... Heat transfer coefficients 30 to 85 W/(m² K).|★★★★★｜蒸汽管回转干燥SAP颗粒的U与热效率量级，可作首版选型依据]|
|Ch6|p.140|数据|性能数据表|Table 6.4 Type1 high-moisture organic: feed 223% d.b., product 11% d.b., heat load 4950 kJ/kg, heating surface 0.34 m²/kg.|★★★★★｜高湿有机物蒸汽管干燥的单位热负荷与换热面，类比SAP高湿进料]|
|Ch6|p.141|数据|双锥真空U与结垢|The overall heat transfer coefficients of this type of dryer, which may vary near 35 W/(m² K)... may drop considerably if the dryer walls are fouled; the values in the range of 5–10 W/(m² K) are not uncommon.|★★★★★｜双锥真空干燥SAP时若壁面结垢/接近Tg，U可由35骤降至5–10，须防]|
|Ch6|p.142|数据|搅拌干燥U|For design purposes the values of the heat transfer coefficients can be assumed to be around 60 W/(m² K) (cone)... around 50 W/(m² K) (paddle)... higher for surface moisture.|★★★★★｜锥形/桨叶接触干燥U=50–60 W/(m²K)，用于SAP/聚合物薄片接触干燥首算]|
|Ch6|p.142|论断|玻璃化转变警告|Materials that may undergo glass transition over temperature ranges encountered may pose unexpected problems... thermal contact resistance between the wall and the material may occur for pasty materials.|★★★★★｜SAP/PP-PE粘合剂接触干燥若壁温接近Tg会软化粘连、接触热阻剧增，是接触干燥头号失效模式]|
|Ch6|p.143|公式|导热设计核心|Q = UA(T₁ − T₂) (6.1)... 1/U = 1/h_h + 1/h_w + 1/h_f + 1/h_m + 1/h_v (6.2).|★★★★★｜接触干燥导热方程与五热阻网络，是SAP/聚合物接触干燥机尺寸计算基础]|
|Ch6|p.143|论断|两主热阻|there are indeed only two main resistances, i.e., the resistance between the wall and the surface of the bed (contact resistance) as well as the resistance across the bed (bulk penetration resistance).|★★★★★｜接触热阻+床层穿透热阻主导，SAP颗粒软化时接触热阻先降后升，须重点管控]|
|Ch6|p.146|公式|吸湿有效热容|c_p,bed,h = c_p,bed + c_p,L X_h (∂X_h/∂T)_p (6.32)... (∂X_h/∂T)_p is the slope of the sorption isobar and X_h is the bound moisture.|★★★★★｜吸湿聚合物接触干燥需引入结合水项，(∂X_h/∂T)_p即Ch.4 GAB等温线对温度的导数]|
|Ch6|p.146|公式|尺寸积分|I = ∫dX/m˙(X) (6.35); A·t_dry = M_dry·I (6.36); A = M˙_dry·I (6.37).|★★★★★｜用干燥速率积分求接触面积，SAP批次/连续接触干燥机尺寸直接算法]|
|Ch6|p.147|论断|恒率/降率选机|During the constant rate period a dryer with a large heating surface is preferred. During the falling rate period a dryer that allows a longer residence time is recommended.|★★★★★｜SAP属降率内部控制，应选长停留、低壁温接触干燥机而非大换热面恒率机]|
|Ch6|p.147|论断|结论能效|Indirect dryers, when they can be used, are thermally more efficient because less or no convective heating medium is required.|★★★★★｜能用接触干燥时thermal效率更高，支撑卫材干燥节能路线选择]|
|Ch7|p.149|论断|并流保护热敏物料|Cocurrent flow is used for heat-sensitive materials even for high inlet gas temperature due to the rapid cooling of the gas during initial evaporation of surface moisture, whereas for other materials countercurrent flow is desirable.|★★★★★｜SAP颗粒/醋酸纤维素薄片属热敏性自由流动固体，并流是高入口温度不降解的唯一安全流型]|
|Ch7|p.150|定义|转鼓干燥机分类|Rotary dryers are classified as direct, indirect–direct, indirect, and special types. This classification is based upon the method of heat transfer being direct when heat is added to or removed from the solids by direct exchange between gas and solids, and being indirect when the heating medium is separated from contact with the solids by a metal wall or tube.|★★★｜自由流动SAP颗粒、PP/PE粒子的后干燥可选直热式；细粉多的超吸水性粉末需防范并流夹带损失]|
|Ch7|p.150|论断|细粉夹带损失|if the solids contain extremely fine particles, excessive entrainment losses in the exit gas stream is possible, due to the large gas volumes and high gas velocities that are, usually, required.|★★★★★｜超吸水性粉末细粉在直热转鼓中易被尾气夹带，设计必须配旋风+袋滤并按细粉率校核]|
|Ch7|p.151|数据|代表性转鼓干燥物料含聚合物|Representative materials dried in direct-heat rotary dryers are sand, stone, ilmenite ore, sodium sulfate, sodium chloride, and fluorspar … cellulose acetate, sodium chloride, styrene, copperas … and urea prills, vinyl resins, oxalic acid …|★★★★★｜醋酸纤维素、苯乙烯、乙烯基树脂被明确列为转鼓干燥物料，证明SAP颗粒(交联聚丙烯酸钠)同属自由流动粒体适用此类干燥机]|
|Ch7|p.152|定义|抄板作用|Their purpose is to lift and shower the solid particles through the gas stream promoting intimate contact between wet solids and hot gases.|★★★★★｜抄板形成'雨幕'是SAP颗粒与热风接触的核心，抄板型线决定干燥均匀性]|
|Ch7|p.152|论断|抄板型线匹配物料|Radial flights with a 90° lip are used for free-flowing materials and flat radial flights without lip for sticky ones.|★★★★★｜SAP颗粒(自由流动)用90°唇径向抄板；潮湿结块料用无唇平板抄板，选型直接指导)|
|Ch7|p.153|数据|最佳装料率|The total amount of solids contained in the drum is about 10–15% of its volume. It has been proved empirically that this loading gives the most efficient performance.|★★★★★｜SAP后干燥转鼓装料应保持10–15%体积分数，过低欠载、过高则物料经窑作用旁通失去热风接触)|
|Ch7|p.154|公式|停留时间定义|The average residence time (or, time of passage) t_t is defined as holdup H divided by the solids feedrate F (7.17).|★★★★★｜停留时间=装料/进料速率，直接决定SAP颗粒终含水规格)|
|Ch7|p.155|论断|不可假设活塞流|it is not safe to assume plug flow of the particles in industrial dryers, because only narrowly sized materials were studied.|★★★★★｜宽粒径SAP颗粒+细粉混合物RTD非活塞流，须以'任一颗粒最大含水'而非均值设规格)|
|Ch7|p.157|公式|体积传热系数|Q = U_a V (Δt)_m (7.30) where Q is the rate of heat transfer … U_a is the volumetric heat transfer coefficient … V is the dryer volume.|★★★★★｜U_a=U·(a/V)统一气体-颗粒接触面积，是SAP转鼓热设计入口量)|
|Ch7|p.158|公式|Ua关联式|U_a = 0.652(n_f − 1)D⁻¹G⁰·⁴⁶ for 6 flights (7.33); U_a = 0.145(n_f − 1)D⁻¹G⁰·⁶⁰ for 12 flights (7.34).|★★★★★｜抄板数6更代表工业转鼓，Ua随气速弱增长，可用于SAP后干燥热功率首算)|
|Ch7|p.159|论断|放大需谨慎|the scale-up of heat transfer data requires caution and experience.|★★★★★｜中试转鼓(壳面积/体积大、热损~15%)到商用放大不可直接外推，SAP线须留余量)|
|Ch7|p.160|公式|Chilton-Colburn类比|The Chilton–Colburn analogy can be expressed as j_H = j_D = f/2 … j_H = St Pr²ᐟ³ (7.42–7.43).|★★★★★｜由传热因子推质因子从而算扩散系数，是转鼓热质同向设计的桥梁(呼应Ch.4 Lewis关系))|
|Ch7|p.161|数据|入口气温与L/D|The inlet gas temperature in a direct-heat rotary dryer is generally fixed by the heating medium, i.e., 400–450 K for steam and 800–1100 K for oil- and gas-fired burners.|★★★★★｜燃气/燃油入口可达800–1100K，SAP颗粒须并流以避高温皮；L/D工业最优4–10)|
|Ch7|p.161|数据|填充率与尺寸比|The volume of the dryer that is filled with material during operation is 10–15%. … the length-to-diameter ratio, L/D, is most efficient between 4 and 10 for industrial dryers.|★★★★★｜装料10–15%、L/D∈[4,10]为SAP后干燥转鼓可行设计带)|
|Ch7|p.162|数据|设备成本|The estimated cost of a steam-heated air rotary dryer … ranges from about $100,000 for a dryer size 1.219 m × 7.62 m to $320,000 for a dryer size 3.048 m × 16.767 m.|★★★★★｜SAP后干燥转鼓capex量级(10–32万美元级)，可用于吸收芯体线投资首估；304不锈钢加价约50%)|
|Ch7|p.162|数据|热效率|High-temperature direct-heat rotary dryers present thermal efficiency in the range 55–75%, which is reduced to 30–55% for dryers that employ steam-heated air as heating medium.|★★★★★｜燃气直热55–75%、蒸汽加热仅30–55%，是SAP颗粒吨能耗成本的核心参数)|
|Ch7|p.163|公式|干燥常数经验式|k_M = k_0 T_0^k1 Y_0^k2 u_0^k3 (7.49) where T_0, Y_0, u_0 are the mean values of air temperature, humidity, and velocity.|★★★★★｜k_M随气温升、湿降、风速升而变，是SAP颗粒薄层干燥动力学首版关联)|
|Ch7|p.164|公式|一级干燥动力学|(X − X_SE)/(X_0 − X_SE) = exp(−k_M t) (7.56) where X is the material moisture content … k_M is the drying constant.|★★★★★｜一级薄层核用于SAP颗粒降速段建模，X_SE为平衡含水由气流水活度决定)|
|Ch7|p.165|公式|几何约束|5% < H/V < 15%; 2 < L/D < 20; 5 < n_f/D < 10.|★★★★★｜装料率、长径比、抄板密度三约束框定SAP后干燥转鼓可行域)|
|Ch7|p.165|论断|设计自由度|A degree of freedom analysis suggests that five design variables are available … T_AC, u, H/V, L/D, and n_f/D.|★★★★★｜五自由度成本最小化模型可直接套用于SAP后干燥转鼓选型优化)|
|Ch7|p.167|公式|热效率定义|η_th = (Q_1 + Q_2 + Q_3 + Q_4 + Q_5)/Q_he (7.90) where Q_1 is the heat for evaporation.|★★★★★｜热效率=蒸发热/总供热，SAP颗粒干燥能耗核算标准式)|
|Ch7|p.168|论断|通用模型存疑|the development of a universal model of the rotary dryer, that combines the cascading motion of the particles with the heat and mass transfer, is questionable.|★★★★★｜转鼓'抄板雨幕+热质传递'耦合仍无通用模型，SAP颗粒设计须依赖中试而非纯理论)|
|Ch8|p.171|论断|FBD处理聚合物/树脂/卫材|Fluidized bed dryers (FBD) are used extensively for the drying of wet particulate and granular materials … They are commonly used in processing many products such as … polymer and resins … healthcare products … detergents and surface-active agents, fertilizers.|★★★★★｜SAP(交联聚丙烯酸钠)属聚合物/树脂/卫材类自由流动粒体，是FBD典型进料)|
|Ch8|p.171|数据|粉末粒径适用窗口|For drying of powders in the particle size range of 50 to 2000 mm [应为µm], fluidized beds compete successfully with other more traditional dryer types.|★★★★★｜SAP颗粒粒径(数十–数百µm)恰在FBD优势区间[原文OCR作mm，依Geldart与上下文应为µm])|
|Ch8|p.172|定义|最小流化速度|A fluidized bed is operated at superficial gas velocities higher than the minimum fluidization velocity, normally at 2–4 u_mf.|★★★★★｜SAP后干燥操作气速取2–4倍u_mf，是流化床设计与放大基础)|
|Ch8|p.173|论断|湿料流化质量差|Particles with high initial moisture content require a higher minimum fluidization velocity … only the top layer of the bed of solids is fluidized. The bottom layers may remain stationary during the initial stage of drying when the solids are quite wet.|★★★★★｜湿SAP颗粒结团仅在顶层流化，须预干燥或振动流化，否则床底滞料)|
|Ch8|p.173|定义|夹带与TDH|This phenomenon is known as elutriation … This point is known as the transport disengagement height (TDH).|★★★★★｜SAP细粉被尾气夹带定义TDH，决定排气口高度以抑细粉流失)|
|Ch8|p.174|定义|Geldart粉体分类|powders can be classified into four groups: group A (aeratable particles, easy-to-fluidize when dry), group B (sandlike …), group C (fine and ultrafine particles, difficult-to-fluidize due to dominated cohesive forces), and group D (large and dense particles, poor fluidization).|★★★★★｜SAP成品为A/B类颗粒含C类细粉、D类粗粒，按类选振动/挡板/喷射流化变体)|
|Ch8|p.175|论断|两阶段喷动-流化床除内部水|In most cases, spray drying alone is not energy efficient to remove all moisture content inside the solids … Fluidized bed drying can be incorporated as the second-stage drying to remove the internal moisture.|★★★★★｜SAP工业标准路线：喷雾成形+流化床除内部结合水，能效最优[原文明示])|
|Ch8|p.175|论断|流化床兼做包衣/造粒/团聚|Beside drying, fluidized bed has found wide ranges of industrial applications … for mixing, dedusting, granulation, coating, agglomeration, cooling … a layer of expensive active agent can be coated on a less expensive substrate.|★★★★★｜SAP抗结块/香精/亲肤包衣可在同一流化床完成，省设备与转运)|
|Ch8|p.176|公式|单颗粒传热系数|h_p = k_g Nu_p / d_p (8.2) where k_g is the gas thermal conductivity, d_p is the particle diameter, and Nu_p is the particle Nusselt number.|★★★★★｜h_p=k_gNu_p/d_p量化SAP颗粒-气体传热，是床层模型入口)|
|Ch8|p.176|数据|界面面积密度与等温假设|The value of heat transfer coefficient of a single particle … is in the range of 1 to 700 W/(m2 K). However, due to the large interfacial surface area, in the order of 3,000 to 45,000 m2/m3, extremely high rates of heat transfer are achieved … an isothermal condition is often assumed.|★★★★★｜3,000–45,000 m2/m3巨大界面面积使SAP颗粒床近等温、传热极快，支撑单相应模型)|
|Ch8|p.177|公式|扩散模型|@X/@t = D(@²X/@r² + 2/r @X/@r) (8.8) where X is the free moisture content, D is the diffusivity (m2/s), and r is radial dimension.|★★★★★｜球坐标Fick扩散模型用于SAP单颗粒降速段，但需以溶胀核替换常数D)|
|Ch8|p.179|公式|经验指数模型|(X − X_eq)/(X_ind − X_eq) = e^(−kt) (8.12) where subscript 'ind' denotes induction period.|★★★★★｜一级指数降湿模型，SAP颗粒干燥常数k须由本厂颗粒实测)|
|Ch8|p.180|论断|离子交换树脂类比|For materials with high mobility of internal moisture such as iron ore, ion-exchange resins, silica gel, most drying takes place close to the distributor plate. Bed height has no effect on its drying rate.|★★★★★｜离子交换树脂(交联聚合物珠)与SAP结构同型，其FBD数据可迁移至SAP颗粒)|
|Ch8|p.180|论断|粒径对干燥时间|For group B particles … drying time … increases as the square of the particle diameter … this effect is much smaller for group A particles.|★★★★★｜SAP颗粒d_p²律意味着收紧粒径分布可显著均匀干燥、减尾部湿粒)|
|Ch8|p.184|论断|多级喷雾流化床=SAP反应器|When solid powders are formed in the spray dryer, these powders still contain some internal moisture … using a second-stage fluidized bed dryer is more cost-effective.|★★★★★｜多级喷雾-流化床(图8.13)即SAP喷粉+流化后干/团聚+冷却产线)|
|Ch8|p.185|论断|振动流化床护脆料|application of fluidized bed can be extended to fragile, abrasive, and heat-sensitive materials. The problem of fine particle entrainment is also avoided.|★★★★★｜SAP颗粒易碎、磨损产尘，振动FBD为低磨耗终处理优选)|
|Ch8|p.187|论断|惰性粒子流化床处理浆料|Drying on inert particles can be performed … ordinary fluidized bed, spouted bed … The liquid to be dried is sprayed into the fluidized bed; it coats the inert particle surfaces.|★★★★★｜SAP浆料或粘合剂溶液可喷入惰性粒子流化床成粉，是一条浆料干燥路线)|
|Ch8|p.189|数据|过热蒸汽FBD干燥纸浆|The application of superheated steam fluidized bed dryer has been reported for drying of paper and pulp, wood-based biofuels … sugar beet pulp, and paddy.|★★★★★｜绒毛浆(纸浆)可用过热蒸汽FBD干燥，无氧化损伤、可灭菌，关联蓬松浆采购)|
|Ch8|p.190|数据|流化床冷冻干燥节能|fluidized bed freeze-drying with adsorbent contributes to about 35% saving in heat requirement.|★★★★★｜热敏生物/卫材粉体可用吸附辅助流化床冷冻干燥，节能35%)|
|Ch8|p.192|论断|浅床plug-flow关联局限|validity of Reay's correlation for particle diffusivity has only been confirmed for bed depths up to 0.10 m … D may be an order of magnitude larger in much deeper beds.|★★★★★｜SAP深床内颗粒扩散系数可能比浅床大一个量级，放大不可简单外推)|
|Ch8|p.193|方法|实验室锚定设计流程|Setup laboratory-scale FBD / Determine fluidization characteristics / Set u_g, z, T_g, perform batch drying / Calculate constant bed temperature batch drying curve / Estimate residence time.|★★★★★｜图8.25实验室→放大流程是卫材实验室自建SAP流化床设计的标配方法)|
|Ch8|p.197|论断|湿表面改变流化质量|The presence of moisture on particle surface can cause major changes to fluidization quality as compared with that of dry particles to which most of the available literature is applicable.|★★★★★｜SAP湿颗粒表面大幅改变流化质量，现有文献多基于干粒，须针对湿SAP补数据)|
|Ch9|p.201|定义|转鼓干燥机适用料|The drum dryer is commonly used to dry viscous, concentrated solutions, slurries or pastes on steam-heated drums.|★★★★★｜高黏度溶液/浆料/膏体(如SAP浆、聚丙烯酰胺凝胶)适合作薄膜传导干燥)|
|Ch9|p.202|论断|高 flux短时可成孔|Exposure of the thin sheet to the high heat flux and high temperature for a short period of time can also impart a porous structure to the dried sheet because of the rapid formation of vapor bubbles within the sheet during 'boiling-like' drying.|★★★★★｜高强热流短时沸腾生成蒸汽泡→多孔片，正是超吸水性/水胶体薄片速溶所需结构)|
|Ch9|p.202|数据|预糊化淀粉应用|It is for these reasons that the drum dryer is widely used around the world in the production of pregelatinized starch for instant food formulations.|★★★★★｜转鼓产预糊化淀粉(多糖凝胶)与SAP同属亲水凝胶加工，机理可迁移)|
|Ch9|p.204|数据|操作窗口|steam pressure ranges from 2 to 7 bar, the drum rotational speed varies from 2 to 30 rpm, the nip width ranges from 0.05 to 0.5 mm, and the ratio of drum rotational speeds is from unity to 5.|★★★★★｜蒸汽压/转速/唇宽操作带是SAP/聚合物凝胶转鼓放大的工艺窗口)|
|Ch9|p.204|论断|进料浓度上限|the feed can be preconcentrated and preheated to reduce the drying load but there is a limit to the feed concentration beyond which the sheet may not form well.|★★★★★｜SAP浆进料浓度过高则薄片不成形，须取下方有成形余量的浓度)|
|Ch9|p.205|论断|须中试不可纯预测|the performance of a drum dryer drying a specific product cannot be adequately predicted by theoretical or semitheoretical models but must be based on drying performance test of the product on a pilot plant drum dryer.|★★★★★｜每种聚合物凝胶须上中试转鼓实测，纯模型不可靠，指导SAP凝胶放大)|
|Ch9|p.205|数据|蒸汽单耗|Typical specific steam consumption … varies from 1.3 to 1.5 kg steam per kg water removed or a steam economy of 0.66 to 0.76 kg water removed per kg steam.|★★★★★｜转鼓蒸汽单耗1.3–1.5 kg蒸汽/kg水，是SAP/聚合物凝胶薄片吨能耗核算基线)|
|Ch9|p.206|论断|淀粉糊化剪切变稀凝胶|The gelatinization of starch slurries into a shear-thinning power law paste also changes the spreading and the mixing regime in the pool … It also swells up several times its original volume.|★★★★★｜淀粉糊化成剪切变稀幂律膏(swell数倍)即水性聚合物凝胶行为，SAP凝胶同理)|
|Ch9|p.207|公式|临界沸腾热流(原文损)|The critical boiling flux, E in W/m2 is given by [Zuber, Eq 9.2] E = 2.177 ρ_v^0.25 (ρ_v−ρ_l)^0.25 … [equation garbled in source].|★★★★★｜核沸腾危机热流限定安全热流上限，防聚合物凝胶膜态沸腾/干斑[公式原文OCR损坏须核对印刷版])|
|Ch9|p.207|数据|施加区传热系数|The heat flux can be as high as 85 kW/m2 … The overall heat transfer coefficient in this zone was reported to be between 2000 and 7000 W/m2 K.|★★★★★｜施加区热流达85 kW/m2、U 2000–7000 W/m2K，远大于对流干燥，决定SAP凝胶表面速定形)|
|Ch9|p.208|数据|薄片区传热系数下降|The overall heat transfer coefficient in this zone [initial sheet] was reported lower … between 600 and 1250 W/m2 K.|★★★★★｜干层绝热使U降至600–1250，聚合物凝胶后段转内部扩散控制)|
|Ch9|p.208|论断|真空转鼓护热敏|A heat-sensitive material dried on a vacuum drum dryer suffers less damage and retains most of its structure and functionality than that dried in ordinary atmospheric drum dryer.|★★★★★｜真空双鼓降T降压保结构功能，适合酶/SAP复合等热敏水凝胶)|
|Ch9|p.209|数据|聚丙烯酰胺可转鼓干|Chemicals that have been successfully dried on a drum dryer are polyacrylamides, and various salts such as silicate, benzoate, propionate, and acetate salts.|★★★★★｜聚丙烯酰胺(丙烯酸盐族亲水聚合物)被明示可转鼓干，证明丙烯酸酯凝胶适于此工艺)|
|Ch9|p.209|论断|热敏药/维生索真空干|Heat-sensitive products such as pharmaceuticals and vitamin-containing products can be dried in a vacuum drum dryer.|★★★★★｜热敏药品/含维生素产品用真空转鼓，关联SAP生物活性包衣的热敏干燥)|
|Ch9|p.210|论断|玻璃化转变粘刀成棒|if the sugar content is high, some of the sugar does not crystallize properly … becomes molten instead at well above the glass transition temperature … form 'sticks' in the final product.|★★★★★｜糖越过Tg呈橡胶态粘刀→'棒'缺陷，普适于SAP粘合剂/非织造涂层的Tg粘辊问题)|
|Ch9|p.210|论断|质量控制靠试错|the set points for the control variables are developed by trial and error for each product … quality … are complex functions of drum speed, temperature, nip width, feed material, feed concentration, and feed-spreading technique.|★★★★★｜每种聚合物凝胶质量由多变量耦合、须试错设参，SAP薄片放大须系统DOE)|
|Ch10|p.213|论断|喷雾干燥定义|Spray drying is a process in which a fluid feed is atomized into droplets that are dried to individual particles when moved in a hot gaseous drying medium, usually air.|★★★★★｜液体进料→单程成粉，正是SAP溶液/胶乳的隔离造粉步骤)|
|Ch10|p.214|案例|聚合物产品清单|Chemical industry, e.g., phenol–formaldehyde resin, catalysts, PVC emulsion-type, amino acids … numerous organic and inorganic chemicals, rubber latex, ceramic powders, detergents, and other products.|★★★★★｜PVC乳液与橡胶胶乳被明确列为喷雾干燥产品，直连SAP/胶乳/表面活性剂粉体)|
|Ch10|p.217|定义|Sauter平均粒径D3,2|The so-called Sauter mean diameter seems to be the most suitable mean value to characterize the droplet cloud together with the size distribution. This is defined as the ratio of the total droplet volume to the total droplet surface … corresponds to the particle diameter with the same volume-to-surface ratio as the entire spray or powder sample.|★★★★★｜D3,2决定干燥速率与最终粒径；聚合物分子量↑→黏度↑→D3,2↑，是SAP细粉/胶乳粉级首要杠杆)|
|Ch10|p.218|定义|粒径分布|The lognormal and Rosin–Rammler distributions are the most common distributions used in the spray drying.|★★★★｜SAP/胶乳粉体粒径分布建模基础；Table 10.3 给出轮式1–600µm、化学品10–1000µm)|
|Ch10|p.220|公式|转盘雾化关联|D_{3,2} = 0.241 N^{-0.26} r_r^{0.26} N_b (10.7) … peripheral velocity ~150 m/s.|★★★★｜转盘雾化粒径关联；SAP溶液黏度高时需校核能否雾化[inference:原文符号OCR部分乱码，常数与指数按上下文还原])|
|Ch10|p.222|数据|气动喷嘴气源|The high cost of compressed air (pressure range, 0.15–0.8 MPa) becomes important to the economics of these nozzles, which have the highest energy consumption of all.|★★★｜气动喷嘴能耗最高，SAP大规模造粉一般不首选)|
|Ch10|p.224|论断|防壁黏/熔点规则|Design of the hot air distribution must prevent [wall impingement] … [air temperature] be lower than the melting point of the product to prevent baking on.|★★★★★｜喷雾干燥壁黏结与热敏SAP/胶乳的熔点/软化温度是首版设计硬约束)|
|Ch10|p.226|定义|气-滴接触方式|There are three basic types of air-droplet contact systems … cocurrent, countercurrent, mixed-flow.|★★★★｜同向流保产品低温(热敏SAP)；逆向流热效率更高，依热敏性择)|
|Ch10|p.233|论断|热效率基准|Spray drying is a very energy-intensive process. There are three main reasons for this: … heat of evaporation in a short time … small temperature difference for heat-sensitive materials … heat lost with exhaust air.|★★★★★｜SAP/胶乳粉体喷雾干燥能耗强度高，是工艺成本核心)|
|Ch10|p.234|定义|系统布局|Spray Dryer Layouts: Open cycle (air + aqueous) … Closed cycle (inert gas + nonaqueous) for evaporation/recovery … Semiclosed (self-inertizing) air with low O2 … Combination Spray and fluidizer: Improved energy consumption, reduced capital costs.|★★★★★｜闭循环处理溶剂型聚合物溶液；喷雾+流化组合是SAP造粒主线)|
|Ch10|p.239|论断|二段喷雾+流化团聚|The semi dried powder is deposited on a moving belt … agglomerates with 'instant' properties.|★★★★★｜喷雾→带式半干→流化床二段团聚，即SAP颗粒后处理模板，桥接Ch8)|
|Ch10|p.241|数据|爆炸数据|PVC — Minimum Explosion Concentration 40 g/m3 (High) … Detergents 170–700 g/m3 (Low).|★★★★｜PVC/洗涤剂粉体爆炸浓度提示SAP/表面活性剂粉体安全设计)|
|Ch10|p.244|定义|产品物性清单|List the dry product properties, such as powder size distribution, particle shape, bulk density, specific heat, glass transition temperature, hygroscopicity, powder flammability.|★★★★★｜玻璃化转变温度被列为喷雾干燥产品选型物性，是聚合物SAP/胶乳的Tg门槛)|
|Ch10|p.245|公式|经验干燥强度|q = (T1+273)^{3.4287} / (T2+273)^{3.34} (10.20) … q is the drying intensity (kg/m3).|★★★★｜离心喷雾干燥强度经验关联，SAP首版尺寸估算可用)|
|Ch10|p.245|公式|单滴扩散模型|@C/@t = (1/r^2) @/@r (r^2 D @C/@r) (10.21) … C is molar concentration of droplet, D diffusion coefficient assumed constant.|★★★★｜降速期单滴干燥扩散方程；但聚合物溶液D随浓度/MW剧变，原假设偏保守[inference])|
|Ch10|p.247|论断|过热蒸汽喷雾|No fire and explosion hazards … No oxidative damage … Better quality product under certain conditions … Closed system operation to minimize air pollution.|★★★★★｜过热蒸汽喷雾去爆炸/氧化损伤，适合氧敏纤维素/淀粉/SAP载体)|
|Ch10|p.247|论断|过热蒸汽局限|Higher product-temperature … Higher capital costs compared to hot-air drying … Possibility of air infiltration.|★★★★｜过热蒸汽路线代价：产品温度高、投资高，需权衡)|
|Ch10|p.249|论断|低露点喷雾|low dew point (LDP) spray dryer, which uses LDP air at near ambient temperature to 80°C, but with very low humidity.|★★★★｜LDP喷雾80°C低温低湿干燥，适合热敏SAP/生化与药品级粉体)|
|Ch10|p.251|论断|喷雾冷冻干燥|Freeze drying (FD) is a significant drying technology for foods, pharmaceuticals, and biochemicals … freeze drying is an order-of-magnitude costlier drying process because of need of refrigeration, vacuum, and long running times.|★★★｜喷雾冷冻造高孔隙球粉，但成本高；对高端SAP/活性粉体为备选)|
|Ch10|p.253|论断|微胶囊化|The active material … is dispersed in a hydrocolloid carrier, e.g., gelatin, modified starch, dextrin or maltodextrin, or gum arabic … homogenized to form an oil-in-water emulsion, and then it is fed to the atomizer for spray drying … entrapped as particles within the hydrocolloid or protein film.|★★★★★｜湿巾/卫生用品活性物的油包水微胶囊化一步法，控释载体)|
|Ch10|p.253|论断|释放控制|The active material from the capsule is released under specified conditions. The main controlling factors of its release are temperature, moisture, and pressure.|★★★★☆｜喷雾干燥造控释装置，正是湿巾/卫生用品活性递送所需机制|
|Ch11|p.255|论断|冷冻干燥定位|Certain biological materials, pharmaceuticals, and foodstuffs, which may not be heated even to moderate temperatures in ordinary drying, may be freeze-dried. As a rule, freeze drying produces the highest quality food product obtainable by any drying method.|★｜仅适用于热敏生物/药品/精致食品，非卫材大宗商品路线)|
|Ch11|p.255|论断|成本高|However, freeze drying is an expensive form of dehydration for foods because of the slow drying rate and the use of vacuum.|★｜慢速+真空=高成本，排除绒毛浆/SAP/无纺布批量干燥)|
|Ch11|p.255|论断|优势|Freeze drying of food and biological materials also has the advantage of little loss of flavor and aroma … minimize the degradative reactions … nonenzymatic browning, protein denaturation, and enzymatic reactions.|★｜风味/活性保留优势对热敏药品有意义，对卫材芯体无此约束)|
|Ch11|p.257|定义|冷冻干燥定义|Freeze drying is a process by which a solvent (usually water) is removed from a frozen foodstuff or a frozen solution by sublimation of the solvent and by desorption of the sorbed solvent (nonfrozen solvent), generally under reduced pressure.|★★｜三阶段(冻结/一次/二次)定义，是热敏高值粉体干燥理论基础)|
|Ch11|p.258|论断|孔隙结构决定速率|The shape of the pores, the pore size distribution, and pore connectivity … depend on the ice crystals that formed during the freezing stage … If the ice crystals are small and discontinuous, then the mass transfer rate of the water vapor in the dried layer could be limited.|★★｜冻结协议决定多孔基质与传质阻力，为高值粉体设计杠杆)|
|Ch11|p.259|数据|结合水比例|The bound moisture is present due to mechanisms of (a) physical adsorption, (b) chemical adsorption, and (c) water of crystallization … about 10–35% of the total moisture content.,卫材相关性★★｜结合水占10–35%，决定二次干燥时间与货架稳定性)

|Ch12|p.288|核心定义|Material classes|Conductors… Insulators… Dielectrics (lossy dielectrics absorb EM energy)… Magnetic compounds|★★★★｜分类指导选材与屏蔽设计|

|Ch12|p.289|核心原则|Self-limitation/leveling|as these liquids are removed the dielectric loss decreases and hence the material heats less well… leads to self-limitation of the heating… great value in obtaining moisture leveling, especially in sheet materials|★★★★★｜自限与均湿，避免局部过干|
|Ch12|p.289|核心定义|Bound vs free water|below the critical moisture content… primarily with bound water; above it we encounter primarily free water… dielectric loss of bound water is very low|★★★★★｜SAP凝胶态属"束缚水"低损耗区|
|Ch12|p.290|公式|Penetration depth|D ¼ l 2 p p 2 … («00=«0) … (12:7); simplified D l 0 p«00 (12:8)|★★★★★｜穿透深度决定厚芯体可用频段|
|Ch12|p.291|关键数据|Penetration table|Depth of penetration Meters 23.9 8.8 4.4 3.0 0.130 0.049 at 5/13.56/27.12/40/915/2450 MHz|★★★★★｜2450MHz仅5cm，厚芯体须用915MHz以下|
|Ch12|p.291|核心原则|Bulk heating|heating with dielectric and microwave energy is, in effect, bulk heating… occurs nearly instantaneously and can be very fast|★★★★｜体积加热机理|

|Ch12|p.301|关键数据|Energy basis|1 kW of microwave or dielectric energy will remove 3.0 lb of water per hour|★★★★｜产能换算基线|

Ch13,p.350,论断,循环混合点前置条件",point 0 ... should be of lower absolute water content than that of the outlet air.,卫材相关性★★｜h-x再循环方向约束，可类比湿度控制)
Ch13,p.351,案例,岩石床储热运行模式",three main modes of operation ... preheated by the collector.,卫材相关性★｜储热模式切换逻辑可借鉴热缓冲)
Ch13,p.353,论断,可再生资产与前景",The renewable nature of solar energy is a definite asset in most parts of the world.,卫材相关性★｜战略层论述，非工艺)
Ch13,p.353,数据,回收期实绩",Some significantly good payback times (1–7 years) have been achieved [14,17,133], mainly with simple and cheap dryers.,卫材相关性★★｜回收期实证支持H11.2)
Ch13,p.354,图表,太阳能干燥物料表",Table 13.4 Solar Drying of Different Materials (crop/timber/fruit).|★｜农业/林产，无卫材条目)|
|Ch14|p.361|论断|喷射床适干热敏固体|Because of the short dwell time in the spout, SBDs can be used to dry heat-sensitive solids, and, using inert solids as the bed material, SBDs have been used successfully to dry pastes, slurries, and heat-sensitive materials.|★★★｜短停留+惰性粒子薄层干燥是热敏SAP/PVA/非织造粘合剂的温和干燥路线，最具卫材价值)|
|Ch14|p.362|定义|经典喷射床局限|The classic or conventional spouted bed (CSB) is a cylindrical vessel with a conical bottom fitted with an inlet nozzle... suffers from limited capacity due to the maximum spoutable bed height and inability to scale up the apparatus beyond 1-m diameter.|★★｜经典喷射床放大受限，放大需导流管，对应卫材粉体线从实验到吨级的放大门槛)|
|Ch14|p.362|定义|导流管作用|The introduction of a hollow, tall vertical tube (draft tube) ... eliminates the former restriction by acting as a pneumatic conveyor.|★★｜导流管解除放大限制，是惰性粒子床干燥器工业化的关键构件)|
|Ch14|p.363|公式|停留时间关系|the mean residence time of the particles is about t_t ≈ H/w_a.|★★｜停留时间≈床高/滑速，是调控热敏材料热剂量的直接杠杆)|
|Ch14|p.366|论断|改性喷射床应用广|various modified SBDs proved to be suitable for energy-efficient drying... for drying of powdered materials (d_p < 100 mm) and of centrifuged materials with high moisture content, as well as coagulating and adhesive pulps, and suspensions.|★★★｜高湿浆/凝乳/粘合浆/悬浮液的节能干燥，与绒毛浆浆料、SAP凝胶、粘合剂乳液同源)|
|Ch14|p.367|论断|播种种子温度上限|the temperature must not exceed 40–45°C... At a drying temperature below 50°C, the drying rate of the seed decreases at first abruptly, then much more slowly. In the temperature range between 46 and 50°C, the aleuron cells form an almost closed, elastic skin in which the diffusion of the moisture is very slow.|★★★｜质量温度窗口逻辑直接可用于设定热敏非织造粘合剂与湿巾乳液/香精涂层的最高颗粒温度)|
|Ch14|p.368|论断|喷射床提速十倍|In a spouted bed dryer using hot air at 45°C, the drying period is reduced to tenfold.|★★｜同等低温下喷射床比静止批式干燥快约十倍，佐证温和干燥亦可高效)|
|Ch14|p.374|定义|惰性粒子干燥原理|The wet solid distributed on the large surface of the inert particles forms a thin layer in which a very short drying process occurs... moisture diffusion resistance in solid can be considered negligible and drying takes place at... relatively low wet bulb temperature.|★★★｜湿料在惰性粒子上成薄层、短程、低湿球温度干燥，是热敏聚合物浆料避免热降解的核心机理)|
|Ch14|p.374|论断|高湿料惰性床优势|For drying of materials of high moisture content, which cannot be directly fluidized or spouted, drying on inert particles can be advantageously applied. The principle of such drying is that the inert particles as an auxiliary phase form the fluidized or spouted bed.|★★★｜无法直接流化/喷射的高湿料用惰性床干燥，类比SAP凝胶、绒毛浆浆料等难以直接干燥的高湿体系)|
|Ch14|p.375|定义|涂覆-干燥-磨损三过程|the partial processes of the inert bed drying occur in the following bed zones: 1. Formation of the coating in the annulus; 2. Drying of the coating in the vicinity of the air inlet; 3. Wearing mainly in the rotation area of the conveyor screw.|★★★｜惰性床干燥三分区过程，是粉体粒径与热剂量协同控制的机理基础，可用于SAP粉体工艺设计)|
|Ch14|p.377|数据|PVC喷射床干燥|Initial moisture content 60% m/m; Temperature of drying air Inlet 120°C, Outlet 60°C; Final moisture content 0.5% m/m; Specific energy consumption 9000 kJ/kg; Specific rate of evaporation 6.4 kg/m²h.|★★★｜PVC聚合物干燥的真实数据：出口仅60°C、终湿0.5%，高能耗因保留原始粒径免粉碎，可作SAP粉体质量-能耗权衡参考)|
|Ch14|p.377|论断|热敏浆料选惰性床|Drying of pastelike materials of high thermal sensitivity such as animal blood and liquid vegetable extracts... the SBD with inert particles, in which the particle temperature stays well below the gas temperature, is considered to be a suitable dryer.|★★★｜颗粒温度远低于气体温度的惰性床是高热敏浆料的适宜干燥器，对应湿巾乳液/香精等热敏涂层的低温干燥需求)|
|Ch14|p.380|论断|适干合成/塑料产品|SBDs are especially suited for drying of heat-sensitive materials with surface moisture, as well as those with bound moisture, such as seeds, foodstuffs, pharmaceuticals, and synthetic products, in one or two stages.|★★★｜明确将合成产品/塑料列为喷射床热敏干燥对象，是卫材聚合物料干燥的权威佐证)|
|Ch14|p.381|论断|塑料用切向进气+螺杆|To dry materials with bound moisture (e.g., plastics), tangential air inlet and an inner transport screw are highly recommended since this way the volumetric gas velocity can be adjusted as required by the drying process regardless of the gas velocity requirement for particle motion.|★★★｜切向进气+内螺杆使颗粒循环与干燥风量解耦，可直接用于热熔粘合剂非织造的固化炉控制)|
|Ch14|p.380|论断|防局部过热与停留可调|Intense particle motion: Good particle mixing prevents localized overheating and ensures the product's uniform moisture content... The residence time of particles may be changed and regulated within very wide limits.,卫材相关性★★★｜强烈混合防局部过热+宽范围可调停留时间，正是避免纤维损伤/卷曲/白度返黄所需的热剂量控制)
Ch14,p.382,论断,新兴技术少工业应用",Spouted beds for drying of granular materials, pastes, and slurries are an emerging technology. Although there are few large-scale industrial applications reported in the literature, the modified spouted beds display significant potential for the future applications.,卫材相关性★★｜喷射床仍属新兴技术、工业放大案例少，卫材采用需中试验证，不应直接放大)
Ch14,p.365,论断,放大关系不适用MSB",Scale-up data of conventional SBDs are well known, but the relations cannot be applied to MSB dryers with inert packing due to different hydrodynamical and drying characteristics.|★★｜常规喷射床放大关系不适用于含惰性填料的MSB，卫材粉体线放大须自行中试，不可套用公式)|
|Ch15|p.383|论断|喷射干燥适用连续薄材|Impinging jets of various configurations are commonly used in numerous industrial drying operations involving rapid drying of materials in the form of continuous sheets (e.g., tissue paper, photographic film, coated paper, nonwovens, and textiles).|★★★★★｜明列纸巾、非织造布、涂布纸、纺织品为喷射干燥主应用，直接对应卫材产线)|
|Ch15|p.383|论断|仅适非结合水+热敏降解风险|Impinging jet drying is recommended only if a major fraction of the moisture to be removed is unbound. If the drying rate is internal diffusion controlled, the high heat transfer rates of the impingement system can often result in product degradation if the product is heat-sensitive.|★★★★★｜核心设计门槛：喷射干燥只去表面/非结合水，内部扩散控制时高热通量会损伤热敏网材，决定绒毛/非织造的干燥分区)|
|Ch15|p.384|论断|薄材高效重材仅去表面|For rapid drying of very thin sheets (e.g., tissue), high-velocity impingement of hot air jets is very effective. On the other hand, for drying of heavier grades of paper and textiles, impingement drying is effective only to remove surface moisture.|★★★★★｜薄纸巾全喷射干燥、厚重非织造/气流成网仅表面脱水，界定穿透干燥与喷射干燥的分工)|
|Ch15|p.384|论断|缺陷:硬化/开裂/条纹/火灾|Extremely high drying rates... may cause such problems as case hardening or cracking due to rapid shrinkage. For combustible products, there is a significant fire hazard unless due care is taken... jet impingement can cause the high-velocity ink or coating to flow, causing undesirable streaks (railroading).|★★★★★｜列举四项卫材网材缺陷（表面硬化、开裂、燃烧、涂布条纹），纸巾属可燃品，湿巾涂布忌条纹)|
|Ch15|p.384|论断|转换行业涂布应用|Another important industrial application of impingement drying is in the printing, packaging, and converting industry, in which printing techniques are used to deposit a thin film of coating onto a moving substrate.|★★★★★｜印刷/包装/转换行业将薄涂层沉积于移动基材，正是湿巾乳液/功能涂层的涂布干燥场景)|
|Ch15|p.385|数据|喷射速度温度范围|Typically, the jet velocity and temperature may range from 10 to 100 m/s and 100 to 350°C, respectively, depending on the product.|★★★★★｜喷射速度10–100 m/s、温度100–350°C的工艺窗口，是纸巾/非织造烘箱设定与安全防护的设计基线)|
|Ch15|p.385|论断|蒸发冷却保表面低温|Because of the extremely high evaporative cooling in the constant rate period, the product surface temperature can be held well below its degradation or ignition temperature.|★★★★★｜恒率期强蒸发冷却使表面远低于降解/燃点，是高温喷射安全干燥纸巾的热力学杠杆)|
|Ch15|p.385|论断|高再循环比|up to 90% of the exhaust air may be recycled after reheating... the jet contact time is too short to utilize the total moisture uptake capacity of the jet. Hence the need for such large recycle ratios.|★★★｜高达90%排气再循环以保热效率，但接触时间短、空气处理昂贵，是能耗与设备成本的权衡点)|
|Ch15|p.386|论断|设计变量过多多靠试错|The design of impingement dryers is both simplified and aggravated by the excessive number of design variables... Most industrial designs are based on more arbitrary and trial-and-error procedures.,卫材相关性★★★｜设计变量多、工业多靠试错，卫材新线须以实验标定而非纯理论放大)
Ch15,p.387,论断,圆孔阵传热更高",according to Pinter and Greimel [5], the heat transfer rates for round hole arrays may be 50–100% higher than those for slot arrays of the same open area.,卫材相关性★★★｜同开孔面积下圆孔阵传热比狭缝阵高50–100%，纸巾Yankee多用穿孔板的经济性依据)
Ch15,p.388,数据,能耗与热效率基准",a well-designed dryer should yield a thermal efficiency of the order of 75–90%. Specific power consumption of 3100 kJ/kg of water evaporated has been reported for air-floater dryers.,卫材相关性★★★★★｜热效率75–90%、比能耗3100 kJ/kg水，是卫材喷射/气浮干燥线能耗成本/吨的核心基准)
Ch15,p.388,论断,窄缝更高传热系数",He found that narrower slots give higher heat transfer coefficients.,卫材相关性★★★｜较窄喷嘴缝给出更高传热系数，指导喷嘴几何选型以提效)
Ch15,p.389,论断,横流使传热减半",Spent flow... must be exhausted properly or the performance of adjoining jets can fall even by a factor of 2. For ASN, the effect of crossflow is severe... No more than two jet rows should be allowed prior to exhaust.,卫材相关性★★★★★｜横流可使相邻射流传热降一半，狭缝阵尤甚，须≤2排即排风，直接决定网材横向水分均匀性)
Ch15,p.389,论断,质量通量降传热",Largemassfluxes... thickening of the thermal–concentration boundary layer and hence a reduction in the heat and mass transfer rates... ~10% may apply in an intense impingement drying of paper.,卫材相关性★★★｜高蒸发通量使边界层增厚、传热降约10%，须对纸张/非织造的Nu/Sh作质量通量修正)
Ch15,p.390,论断,抽吸协同增效",Application of suction increases the impingement heat transfer rate... a 10–15% increase in the average impingement heat or mass transfer rate may account for the suction effect.|★★★★★｜喷射+穿透干燥(抽吸)协同增10–15%传热，是热敏非织造可透网材降率期的最佳组合)|
|Ch15|p.391|论断|非均匀阵列调降率期|the use of nonuniform arrays... can be tailored to meet the requirements of varying drying rates. This is particularly useful for drying in the falling rate period of heat-sensitive products.,卫材相关性★★★★★｜非均匀喷嘴阵列可针对降率期热敏产品定制干燥速率，是卫材网材分级干燥的直接手段)
Ch15,p.392,论断,高温喷射关联缺失",Very little information exists for the impingement heat transfer coefficient with high temperature jets... measured heat transfer coefficients tend to be somewhat lower than those predicted by Martin's correlations over the temperature range of 100 to 400°C.|★★★｜100–400°C高温喷射实测传热低于Martin关联，纸巾高温烘箱须实验标定而非套用关联)|
|Ch16|p.395|论断|短接触时间-低物料温|One of the features of these types of dryers is the relatively short contact time between the hot air and the particulate materials (0.5–10 s) at the drying section. Because of this the material temperature stays always low in the drying process|★★★｜秒级接触使粉体/聚合物粒料温度低，SAP干粉与PP/PE母粒可避免热降解)|
|Ch16|p.395|论断|表面水适用边界|flash dryers are most suitable for removal of external moisture (surface moisture) and are less suitable for removal of internal moisture|★★★★｜闪蒸仅除表面/非结合水；SAP干粉/聚合物粒料适配，绒毛浆/非织造网(含结合水)不适用)|
|Ch16|p.395|数据|粒径范围(单位存疑)|The size of particulates to be dried is usually in the range of 10–500 mm|★★★｜原文单位mm与闪蒸粉体实践不符(疑为µm)，SAP/聚合物粉体粒径设计输入须按µm校读)|
|Ch16|p.396|论断|热敏料优势|Short contact time and parallel flow make possible to dry thermolabile materials|★★★★｜并流+短停留保护热塑性聚合物粉体不过热降解)|
|Ch16|p.396|定义|自由沉降速度约束|The gas velocity must be greater than the free fall velocity of the largest particle to be dried|★★★｜决定管径与风量，直接关联风机能耗与放大)|
|Ch16|p.397|论断|粉体爆炸红线|There is a risk of fire and explosion, so care must be taken to avoid flammability limits in the dryer|★★★★★｜纤维素/SAP粉尘爆炸红线，粉体干燥安全硬约束)|
|Ch16|p.397|论断|毒性料限制与过热蒸汽替代|Because of powder emission, the dryer cannot be used for toxic materials. In some cases this disadvantage can be avoided using superheated steam as a drying agent|★★★★｜SAP非毒但可燃粉尘，过热蒸汽可消除爆炸风险)|
|Ch16|p.397|论断|进口温度受固热敏约束|the temperature of the hot gas should be as high as possible without exceeding limits imposed by the thermal sensitivity of the solids or safety considerations|★★★★｜进口温度上限应由聚合物Tg/热降解温度硬约束(见POP块))|
|Ch16|p.399|论断|过热蒸汽优势|Flash dryers using superheated steam as drying medium instead of air have some advantages such as no fire or explosion risk and higher efficiency (if exhaust steam is utilized elsewhere in the process)|★★★★★｜替代空气消除SAP粉尘爆炸风险，余热可回收则效率更高)|
|Ch16|p.399|论断|过热蒸汽局限|The limitations of using superheated steam as drying medium are that the system itself and the operation of the system are more complex. Leaks are prohibited as noncondensables cause problems with energy recovery|★★★｜系统气密性要求高，进料/排料禁空气渗入，启动停机更复杂)|
|Ch16|p.399|定义|旋闪干燥器|the spin-flash dryer is basically a mechanically agitated fluidized bed device for very short residence times... suited for drying sludge, pulps, pastes, filter cakes, high viscosity liquids, without the use of an atomizer|★★★｜膏/浆/滤饼/高粘SAP浆免雾化干燥，可替代喷雾干燥省能耗)|
|Ch16|p.399|数据|旋闪产能|Numerous materials have been dried successfully in such units at capacities up to 10 tons/h|★★｜产能量级参考(10 t/h))|
|Ch16|p.400|论断|聚合物行业应用|Flash dryers are widely used in the plastic and polymer industries|★★★★★｜PVC/塑料粒子工业化闪蒸，直接对应卫材聚合物粒料干燥)|
|Ch16|p.401|案例|PVC粒子建模|Andrieu and Bressat [16] presented a simple model for pneumatic drying of PVC particles|★★★★★｜热塑性聚合物闪蒸直接案例，进口温度受Tg约束)|
|Ch16|p.402|论断|仅表面水可干|only products containing surface moisture can be dried in flash dryers. Drying of heat-sensitive products in this type of dryers is very useful,卫材相关性★★★★｜重申表面水限制+热敏料优势，界定卫材粉体适用边界)
Ch16,p.402,论断,作复杂系统干燥段",Flash dryers can be used as a drying stage in more complex systems... as a predrying stage to a fluidized bed dryer,卫材相关性★★｜可作流化床/喷雾前预干段，组合工艺思路)
Ch16,p.403,论断,模型须实验验证",It should be kept in mind that the developed mathematical model should be experimentally validated in order to use it as a design tool,卫材相关性★★★｜放大前须用中试数据校验两相流模型)

|Ch16|p.403|公式|两相流动量方程|∂(ε_k ρ_k V_k)/∂t + ∇·(ε_k ρ_k V_k V_k) = −∇(ε_k P_k) + ∇·τ_k + ε_k ρ_k g + M_kj + S_k V_k  (16.6),卫材相关性★★★｜气固耦合动量，预测压降与停留)
Ch16,p.404,公式,能量(焓)方程",∂(ε_k ρ_k h_k)/∂t + ∇·(ε_k ρ_k V_k h_k) = ε_k ∂P_k/∂t + τ_k:∇V_k − ∇·q_k + Q_k + Q_kj + S_k h_kj  (16.15),卫材相关性★★★｜能量平衡，联通温度场与相变)
Ch16,p.404,公式,相间换热",Q_gs = 6 ε_s (1−ε_s) λ_g (T_g − T_ss)/d_s²  (16.16),卫材相关性★★★｜气固换热率，决定干燥速率)
Ch16,p.404,公式,阻力系数",C_D = max[24(1+0.15Re^0.687)/Re, 0.44]  (16.13),卫材相关性★★｜单颗粒阻力，关联式基础)

|Ch17|p.409|论断|穿透流通式定义|Product is carried through the dryer on conveyors and hot air is forced through the bed of product. It is often described as simply a conveyor in a box with hot air|★★★★★｜热风强制穿透可透床层=穿透空气(TAD)干燥/热粘合，正是气流成网与非织造粘合烘箱的本质单元操作)|
|Ch17|p.409|论断|适用粒径与不可透片材排除|the conveyor dryer is best suited for drying particulate material in the 1- to 50-mm diameter range... impermeable sheeted materials or slurries are not well suited to conveyor drying,卫材相关性★★★★★｜限可透床层、排除不透气片材，界定穿透干燥与喷射/杨克式分工；多孔气流成网网材正属可透床层)
Ch17,p.410,论断,玻化转变温度淬火",If cooling to a temperature below ambient is required (for example to set a coating by getting it below its glass transition temperature), chilled air is used as the cooling medium,卫材相关性★★★★★｜Tg淬火锁定涂层/双组分皮芯粘合点，热粘合成网冷却段的直接聚合物物理依据)
Ch17,p.410,数据,典型干燥时间5-240min",typical drying times are 5 to 240 min. Drying times significantly longer than this typically result in such a large conveyor dryer, that the purchase price is difficult to justify,卫材相关性★★★★｜停留时间5–240min量级，界定非织造穿透烘箱节拍与占地/造价权衡)

Ch17,p.411,论断,单程多段最通用",The single pass/multiple-stage dryer configuration is the most versatile dryer configuration available,卫材相关性★★★★｜可分区控温/风速/床深，最适非织造多级热粘合烘箱)
Ch17,p.412,论断,多程最流行",the multiple pass dryer is the most popular conveyor dryer arrangement found in many industries,卫材相关性★★★｜叠层多程占地小、灵活，参考非织造线紧凑布置)
Ch17,p.413,论断,开孔率临界与堵塞",Choosing the correct perforation size and percent open area or mesh size is critical... A plugged bed will not allow air to flow through, therefore drying rate and uniformity will suffer,卫材相关性★★★★★｜开孔率/网孔堵塞直接决定穿透均匀性，对应网材穿孔板设计与网孔堵死缺陷)
Ch17,p.413,定义,床材不锈钢",Products that are destined for human consumption or that are corrosive typically require stainless steel construction. AISI 300 series stainless steels such as 304 or 316,卫材相关性★★★｜卫材/食品级须304/316不锈钢，卫生设计选材依据)
Ch17,p.415,论断,铺料均匀性=质量",If the feeding or spreading is not right, the dryer will not be able to dry the product uniformly. The end result will be poor product quality, wasted energy, and reduced capacity,卫材相关性★★★★★｜喂料/铺料不均是横向水分条斑根因，非织造粘合烘箱铺网质量前置约束)
Ch17,p.415,论断,浅区过干团块夹湿",air will flow preferentially through the shallow areas... over dried... clumps... air will tend to flow around clumps rather than through them. This results in wet product inside the clumps,卫材相关性★★★★★｜优先流短路→薄区过干、团块夹湿，正是网材横截面水分不均与缺陷机理)
Ch17,p.416,论断,理论模型不能替代试验",Although it would be nice to develop a generic theoretical model of conveyor drying that covers all products, theoretical modeling will never replace testing or previous experience,卫材相关性★★★｜放大须中试/经验标定，穿透烘箱不可纯理论放大)
Ch17,p.416,公式,体积传热系数",Q = h_v · V · ΔT where h_v is the volumetric heat transfer coefficient (kW/m3·°C) and V is the volume of the product bed (m3),卫材相关性★★★★★｜以床体积+体积传热系数h_v sizing，避开难定义表面积，非织造穿透线直接可用)
Ch17,p.416,论断,床层渗透率非线性",the permeability of a bed of product will often not change linearly with increasing bed depth... suddenly drop as the particles compress together and begin to blind over the bed,卫材相关性★★★★｜湿网/气流成网压实致渗透率骤降、孔盲死，是热粘合段核心难点)
Ch17,p.417,图表,表17.1停留时间",Typical Retention Times... Synthetic rubber 8–15%→0–1%, 20–60 min, 80–120°C; Coated cereal 5–20 min, 100–150°C,卫材相关性★★★★｜合成橡胶/涂布谷物停留温度区间，类比热粘合/涂布非织造工艺窗口)
Ch17,p.417,图表,表17.2热平衡",Typical Heat Balance... Total heat consumption 1831 kW (to evaporated water 1263, exhaust air 383, product 145, losses 40),卫材相关性★★★★★｜四项式热平衡范例，蒸发水占~69%，能耗成本/吨基线)
Ch17,p.418,论断,蒸发冷却保低温",the product in the dryer is typically at a much lower temperature than the air temperature due to evaporative cooling... near or approaching bone dry... the product may approach the air temperature,卫材相关性★★★★★｜恒率期蒸发冷却使网材远低于气温，近绝干时表面升温跨入降解/硬化窗口)
Ch17,p.419,论断,湿度过低致表面硬化",If the humidity level in the dryer is too low, energy consumption may be excessive and with some products, casehardening may be a problem,卫材相关性★★★★★｜区湿度过低→能耗高且表面硬化(案例硬化)，非织造网材表面硬化缺陷控制点)
Ch17,p.419,论断,热回收>10%",such heat recovery systems can often reduce total energy consumption by over 10%,卫材相关性★★★★★｜预热补风/排风余热回收可降总能耗>10%，能耗成本直接杠杆)

Ch17,p.421,论断,过干即减产损失",Even a 0.5% loss in production due, for example, to poor moisture uniformity represents a larger cost item than the fuel cost. If the product is overdried... this represents a loss in production since drying removes weight from a product that is typically sold by weight|★★★★★｜过干即减重即损失(按重计价)，水分不均>燃料成本，非织造线控水即控利润)|

Ch18,p.422,论断,不透明体表面薄层吸收",Generally solid bodies absorb all of the radiation in a very narrow layer near the surface. This is a very important consideration in modeling the heat transfer process,卫材相关性★★★★★｜不透明网材仅表面薄层吸收入射，IR是表面固化工具、穿透浅)
Ch18,p.422,公式,基尔霍夫/维恩/斯特藩",r + a + t = 1 (18.1); λ_max T = 2897.6 µm·K (18.3); E_b = σT^4 (18.4),卫材相关性★★★｜辐射传输三方程，发射器温度→峰值波长设计基础)
Ch18,p.423,公式,灰体净辐射",Q_r = σ(T_i^4 − T_j^4) / (1/ε_i A_i + F_ij(1/ε_j A_j − 1))  (18.8),卫材相关性★★★｜两灰体净辐射换热，发射器功率与视角因子 sizing)

Ch18,p.425,论断,发射温取舍穿透与焦痕",choose the heat source at 2500 K... allow the radiation to penetrate... if the lower heat source temperature is used, the surface may be damaged (scorched),卫材相关性★★★★★｜较高发射温反而穿透好、低温源表面焦痕，热敏涂层防焦设计要点)
Ch18,p.425,论断,厚湿材宜高透过避表损",in drying thick moist materials such as foodstuffs, it is preferable to use a material with high transmissivity to avoid extremely intensive heating and thermal damage of the surface,卫材相关性★★★｜厚湿材宜高透过率避表面热损，厚非织造/芯体IR穿透受限)
Ch18,p.426,论断,仅改能量平衡",Only the energy balance equation must be modified to include IR in the calculation procedure for drying... whereas the mass balance equation remains the same,卫材相关性★★★｜IR建模仅能量方程增辐射项，质量平衡同常规干燥，放大可直接套用)
Ch18,p.427,论断,不透明体表面窄吸收",an 'opaque' solid absorbs radiation in a narrow zone near the surface rather than attenuating within its volume,卫材相关性★★★★｜再次确认IR表面吸收本质，界定其仅涂层/表面固化能力)
Ch18,p.428,论断,优势:水分剖面均化",Uniform heating of the product... Leveling of the moisture profiles in the product and low product deterioration,卫材相关性★★★★★｜IR均匀加热并均化水分剖面、低劣化，对应网材横截水分均匀与低损伤)
Ch18,p.428,论断,解决VOC携带难题",IR drying offers solutions to problems... associated with the carrying of volatile organic compounds from solvent-based paints by the exhaust hot air in conventional convective dryers,卫材相关性★★★★｜IR解决溶剂型涂层VOC被排气带走难题，涂布湿巾环保合规)
Ch18,p.429,论断,局限:本质表面干燥",Essentially surface dryers. Nevertheless, a great effort is done to improve this technology in order to adapt it to drying thick materials,卫材相关性★★★★｜IR本质表面干燥，厚材/蓬松芯体穿透弱，须诚实界定能力边界)

Ch18,p.429,论断,薄材/纸/膜表面干燥",IR heating is widely used in industry for surface drying or dehydration of thin sheets such as textiles, paper, films, paints, stove enamels,卫材相关性★★★★★｜薄材/纸/膜/织物表面干燥，非织造湿巾基材正属此类)

Ch18,p.432,论断,间歇IR+连续对流",combination of intermittent IR radiation with continuous convection heating... for drying thick porous materials such as panels made of wood and of acoustic tiles,卫材相关性★★★｜间歇IR+连续对流用于厚多孔材，厚非织造/芯体干燥方向)

Ch19,p.439,论断,可转任意直接干燥器",any direct dryer, in principle, can be converted to superheated steam operation (e.g., flash, fluid bed, spray, impinging jet, conveyor dryers, etc.),卫材相关性★★★★★｜闪蒸/流化/喷雾/喷射/输送带均可转蒸汽介质，对应Ch16/17/18设备)

|Ch22|p.547|论断|盐渍=渗透脱水有平衡终点|Salting converts fresh fish into shelf-stable products by reducing the moisture content, and acting as a preservative... Equilibrium is the end point of osmotic process, i.e., the net rate of mass transport is zero.|★★★｜渗透脱水向净传质为零的平衡收敛，是SAP/纸浆平衡含水率(第24章)的概念先驱)|

|Ch22|p.550|论断|表面硬壳=硬化降速须避免|Heating the surface too fast can produce a hard crust (mostly dried soluble protein), which retards movement of moisture. This phenomenon (case-hardening) can severely reduce the rate of drying and must be avoided.|★★★★｜明确定义表面硬化(case-hardening)及其成因与降速后果，直接对应热风非织造网材的表层硬化/开裂缺陷)|
|Ch22|p.550|论断|干燥降水分活度保藏|Drying reduces the water activity, thus preserving foods by avoiding microbial growth and chemical reactions causing deterioration.|★★｜低水分活度抑制微生物与化学反应，是干燥保藏通用原理，与卫材仓库防潮同源)|
