|Ch22|p.552|论断|高温杀微生物vs低温保品质|The highest possible drying temperatures should be used to maximize thermal death even though low drying temperatures are best for maintaining organoleptic characteristics. Other alternative is to use high drying temperature initially at high moisture content and then drying at low temperature.|★★★｜品质-速率权衡：高湿恒率段高温、低湿降率段低温，正是卫材线恒率→降率分段控温的翻版)|
|Ch22|p.552|数据|干燥品质特征表|Quality Characteristics of Dried Foods: Microbial (Pathogens, Spoiling, Toxin), Chemical (Browning, Oxidation, Colorloss, Aroma loss), Physical (Solubility, Texture, Rehydration, Porosity, Shrinkage, Pores' characteristics), Nutritional (Vitamin loss, Protein loss, Fatty acid loss, Functionality loss).|★★｜干燥品质四维分类(微生物/化学/物理/营养)可作为卫材干燥品质评审清单的模板)|
|Ch22|p.553|数据|单分子层aw≈0.3抗氧化最强|At water activities around the monolayer (aw ≈ 0.3), resistance to oxidation is greatest.|★★★｜单分子层水分活度最小氧化窗口，是SAP/纸浆仓储防潮目标(第24章量化)的定性前身)|
|Ch22|p.553|论断|褐变集中于干燥中段|Browning tends to occur primarily at the center of drying period... product temperature rises... less evaporative cooling is taking place.,卫材相关性★★｜中段褐变因蒸发冷却减弱、产品升温，提示恒率→降率转折处须控温，类比热敏涂层)
Ch22,p.554,论断,玻璃化转变解释收缩坍塌",The glass transition theory is one of the proposed concepts to explain the process of shrinkage and collapse during drying... there is negligible collapse (more pores) in material if processed below glass transition and higher the difference between the process temperature and the glass transition temperature, the higher the collapse.|★★★★｜以玻璃化转变(Tg)解释干燥收缩/孔隙坍塌，是聚合物理(odTg)与SAP/粘结剂网材蓬松度控制的真实桥梁)|
|Ch22|p.554|论断|冻干多孔热风密实|In freeze-drying, since the temperature of drying is below Tg′ ... the material is in the glassy state. Hence shrinkage is negligible... very porous. In hot air drying... above Tg′ ... rubbery state and substantial shrinkage occurs... dense and shriveled.|★★★★｜冻干(玻璃态多孔)vs热风(橡胶态密实)对照，正是卫材气流成网保持蓬松vs热风过烘塌芯的机理)|
|Ch22|p.554|数据|鱼/肉Tg′范围|The values of Tg′ for fish and meat varied from 11 to 15°C.|★★｜生物基质Tg′量级参考；SAP/纤维素粘结剂Tg更高，但同属玻璃态-橡胶态框架)|
|Ch22|p.555|公式|表观收缩模型|V_a/V_0 = (1−ε_ex−ε_a)/(1−ε_ex0−ε_a0) where V_a is the apparent volume.|★★｜多孔基质表观体积收缩的通用模型，可借用于干燥网材的尺寸变化评估)|
|Ch22|p.556|公式|鱿鱼孔隙率关联|ε_op = 0.079 + 0.164y − 0.099y², ε_cp = 0.068 + 0.216y − 0.138y² (y = X_w/X_w0).|★｜鱿鱼/枪乌贼的经验孔隙率关联，仅作方法示范，数值不可移植到卫材)|
|Ch22|p.556|论断|再水合部分不可逆|rehydration cannot be considered simply as a process reversible to dehydration.,卫材相关性★★｜干燥引起的结构改变(收缩/玻璃化)多不可逆，提示卫材干燥工艺参数一旦设定难回退)
Ch22,p.557,论断,干燥发展史定位",In this chapter a brief overview of fish drying is presented. The main focus is given to drying methods, pretreatment, and quality characteristics.|★｜综述定位，明确本章为鱼干燥方法/预处理/品质概览，无卫材直接内容)|
|Ch23|p.561|论断|低水分是安全储粮前提|Grain needs low moisture levels for safe storage. Drying has always been the most common method of preserving grain.|★★★｜低水分保藏为通用原理，对应卫材绒毛/SAP的残水规格与仓库防潮)|
|Ch23|p.562|论断|通风须看环境温湿度|the operator should select days when the average relative humidity is less than or equal to 70% [4] and the air temperature is more than 1.1°C (30°F) above ambient.|★★★｜环境RH≤70%、气温高于露点1.1°C的选时规则，是仓库平衡调湿(第24章ERH)的操作翻版)|
|Ch23|p.562|数据|常规通风风量|The recommended airflow rate for normal aeration of shelled corn, soybeans, and small grains at 125 Pa (0.5 in. of water) is 5 m3/h per m3 of grain (0.1 cfm/bu).|★★｜通风风量参考量级，仅作方法示范，数值不可移植到卫材)|
|Ch23|p.563|论断|多级干燥=高温+通风/自然风|The term multistage drying refers to any process that uses high-temperature drying in combination with aeration or natural-air drying.|★★★★｜多级干燥框架：高温段+低温通风段，正是绒毛浆包/卷后调湿(回性)的工艺逻辑)|
|Ch23|p.563|论断|干化法两段调湿|Dryeration is the term referring to the two-stage process by which grain is dried in a heated-air dryer to within about 2% of its 'dry' moisture content and then moved to and stored in an aerating bin for about 10 h [3]. This allows time for moisture within the kernels to move to the outside for easier removal.|★★★★★｜干化法(dryeration)的'回性'机理——干燥后静置使内部水分外移——直接对应绒毛浆包出干燥机后的平衡调湿)|
|Ch23|p.563|数据|干化法增益|Capacity increases of up to 60% of the grain drying system are possible... fuel savings of 20% or more.|★★★★｜回性使产能+60%、燃料-20%+，量化佐证'先近目标后回性'策略的能耗/产能收益)|
|Ch23|p.563|数据|组合干燥节能|combination drying... energy requirements may be reduced by as much as 50%.|★★★｜高温到19–23%再自然风仓干燥节能达50%，佐证多级干燥的能效优势)|
|Ch23|p.564|数据|安全储藏水分与最高温度表|maximum moisture content of crop for safe storage... 13 13 13(Seed wheat,12%)... Maximum safe temperature of heated air... Seed 43°C, Commercial 54°C, Animal feed 82°C.|★★★★｜安全储藏水分(11–13%wb)与按用途的最高安全温度(43/54/82°C)双护栏，可类比卫材残水规格+热损伤温度窗)|
|Ch23|p.564|论断|长期储藏水分再降|The products are to be stored for long periods, the moisture contents should be 1–2% lower than shown in this tabulation.|★★★｜长期储藏水分再降1–2%，提示卫材仓库长期存放应把残水规格收严)|
|Ch23|p.565|论断|新干粮入储须冷却|Before storing, newly dried grain must be cooled.|★★｜出干燥机后须冷却再储，避免热芯在仓内回潮/变质，类比卫材网材冷却)|
|Ch23|p.566|论断|窄柱降水分差|the moisture differential across the grain column is lowered as its width is decreased. A thinner column therefore means that, for a given average moisture content, the inner layer is less overdried.,卫材相关性★★★｜减薄料层降低内外水分差、减少过干，是连续干燥机/气流成网厚度设计的通用原则)
Ch23,p.569,论断,薄层无水分梯度",the layer of grain that is dried is thin (less than about 0.20 m), no significant moisture gradient develops through the grain [8]. This means that the drying temperature is limited only by the possibility of heat damage to the grain.|★★★★★｜薄层(<0.20m)无显著水分梯度=薄层干燥前提，正是第24章薄层干燥模型的建模基础；绒毛浆包属厚床需回性)|
|Ch23|p.569|论断|深床干燥达3.5m|Deep-layer dryers consist of silo bins... Depths of up to 3.5 m of grain may be dried at one time [8].|★★★｜深床(料层达3.5m)干燥存在梯度，绒毛浆包/卷是深床，须用回性消除梯度)|
|Ch23|p.570|论断|热泵低温节能|Heat pump dehumidifiers in drying equipment have been shown to offer many benefits... drying at lower temperatures, lower cost and operation even under humid ambient conditions.|★★★｜热泵除湿低温干燥，对热敏卫材与高湿环境的干燥线有参考价值)|
|Ch24|p.573|论断|谷物油料年产量巨大需储运|The world produces annually about 2 billion tonnes (Gt) of grains and oilseeds [1] that are handled and stored... To design handling, inspection, and storage systems... properties of interest are: bulk and particle densities, porosity... equilibrium moisture content (EMC), specific heat, thermal conductivity, dielectric constant...|★★｜开篇列出设计干燥/储运所需物性清单，其中EMC/导热/比热正是卫材干燥与仓库设计需测的同类参数)|
|Ch24|p.578|论断|湿基干基必须明示|In theories of grain drying, the moisture contents of samples are usually expressed on a dry mass basis (db)... it is suggested that the scientific community adopt a convention to report moisture contents on wet basis in percentage and moisture contents on dry basis in decimal fraction.|★★★★★｜强制声明湿基(wb%)与干基(db小数)，是绒毛浆/SAP残水规格与QC证书的必备纪律，避免数值歧义)|
|Ch24|p.579|论断|EMC静态盐溶液测定法|a sample of known mass is allowed to reach equilibrium with air maintained at a constant relative humidity and temperature. The moisture content of the sample at equilibrium is measured and is defined as the EMC. The constant relative humidity environments are usually created using saturated salt solutions in containers.|★★★★★｜EMC操作定义+饱和盐溶液静态法，正是测定绒毛浆/SAP解吸等温线、设定仓库RH目标的实验流程)|
|Ch24|p.580|论断|吸着EMC低于解吸EMC滞后|The sorption EMC is lower than the desorption EMC at the same relative humidity because of the hysteresis phenomenon that is exhibited by biological materials.|★★★★★｜吸/解吸滞后：同种RH下解吸含水更低；干燥须按解吸支设定出机残水，仓库回潮会向较高吸着支爬升)|
|Ch24|p.580|论断|五等温线方程GAB最优|The modified Henderson [15,16] and modified Chung–Pfost... adopted as standard equations... The G.A.B. equation has recently been recognized as the most satisfactory theoretical isotherm equation, but it does not incorporate the effect of temperature.|★★★★★｜五种等温线(Modified Henderson/Chung–Pfost/Halsey/Oswin/GAB)中GAB被认作最优理论模型，用于拟合纤维素/SAP等多层水吸附)|
|Ch24|p.580|公式|薄层干燥方程24.5|M = M_e + (M_i − M_e) exp(−K t^N) (24:5)|★★★★★｜薄层干燥/回潮指数逼近方程：M_e平衡含水、M_i初含水、K干燥常数(1/s)、N系数，可直接拟合绒毛浆层/SAP颗粒干燥动力学)|
|Ch24|p.580|论断|动态法测ERH更快|In the dynamic method... the time to reach equilibrium is reduced to 6–12 h depending on the conditions of the sample.|★★★｜动态(再循环空气)法6–12h得ERH，比静态数周更快，适合卫材研发快速测a_w-含水曲线)|
|Ch24|p.580|论断|吸附机理含化学/物理吸附|The moisture uptake can be by chemisorption, adsorption, absorption, or a combination as moisture content increases. The term sorption includes all mechanisms of moisture uptake.|★★★｜水分摄取含化学吸附/吸附/吸收，'sorption'统括——提示SAP/纤维素的吸水是多层物理+部分化学键合，非单一机制)|
|Ch24|p.580|数据|等温线常数表24.9|Constants of Selected Equations for the Isotherm of Various Seeds... Barley PF 475.12 0.14843 71.996; Corn HE 6.6612E-05 1.9677 42.143...|★｜谷物等温线常数仅作方法示范，数值不可移植到卫材，须为浆/SAP重新拟合)|
|Ch24|p.581|论断|GAB加温度修正|Jayas and Mazza [24] modified the G.A.B. equation by dividing the constant C by temperature to give a three-parameter equation.|★★★★｜Jayas–Mazza将GAB常数C除以温度得三参数式，弥补基础GAB不含温度的缺陷，使a_w-含水模型随温度可用)|
|Ch24|p.582|论断|湿物料比热=干基+水贡献|Specific heat of a moist agricultural product can be related to its dry, c_db, or wet, c_wb, mass... c_db = c_d + c_w·M_db.|★★★｜湿物料比热=干物质比热+水比热×含水，提示干燥湿浆比干浆需更多显热，影响烘箱能耗估算)|
|Ch24|p.589|论断|汽化潜热随含水降低而升|As the moisture content is lowered, there is an increase in the amount of energy required to evaporate the water molecules in seeds.|★★★★★｜含水越低移除单位水所需能量越高，正是'最后几个水分点最难去除'(第23章)的物理根源，量化末段干燥能耗)|
|Ch24|p.589|公式|滞后汽化潜热方程24.32|h*_fg = h_fg [1 + a exp(−b M)] (24:32)|★★★★｜h*_fg=h_fg[1+a·exp(−bM)]量化结合水额外汽化能，a,b按作物列于表24.14，可用于末段干燥能耗模型)|
|Ch24|p.590|论断|呼吸热致储藏产热|Under storage conditions grains generate heat as a result of respiration and mold activity... wet pockets... favorable environment for growth of microorganisms and excessive heat generation.|★★★｜储藏中呼吸/霉变产热形成湿芯热点，类比仓库中高湿卫材包回潮后的微生物风险与热点)|
|Ch24|p.598|论断|符号总表|List of Symbols: M moisture content db, kg H2O/kg dry matter; M_e equilibrium moisture content; M_i initial moisture content; RH relative humidity, decimal; K drying constant, 1/s; N drying coefficient.|★★★★｜全书符号表统一M/M_e/M_i/RH/K/N定义，是薄层方程与等温线跨章调用的基准，卫材建模应沿用)|
|Ch25|p.625|定义|Texture irreversible damage|The problem of hot air drying ... is the irreversible damage to the texture, leading to shrinkage, slow cooking, and incomplete rehydration.|★★★｜热风干燥致纤维/涂层收缩与表面硬化类比，警示过度干燥脆裂|
|Ch25|p.626|定义|Pectin rehydration role|Levi et al. [84] observed that pectin, one of the major cell wall and intercellular tissue components, played a significant role in the rehydration capacity of dehydrated fruits.|★★★★｜果胶多糖网络维持再水合能力，类比交联SAP凝胶持水骨架|
|Ch25|p.626|公式|Water activity a_w|It is defined as the ratio of the vapor pressure of water P in the food to the vapor pressure of pure water P0 at the same temperature (a_w = P/P_0).|★★★★★｜a_w是底物无关的稳定性主变量，直接用于浆/SAP微生物规格|
|Ch25|p.626|论断|Microbial a_w threshold|microorganisms cannot grow in the dehydrated food system when the water activity range is less than or equal to 0.6–0.7|★★★★★｜设定仓库回潮与收货水分的微生物安全界线|
|Ch25|p.626|定义|Sorption isotherm|The relationship between equilibrium moisture content and water activity, known as the sorption isotherm, is an important characteristic that influences many aspects of dehydration and storage.|★★★★★｜吸着等温线方法（见Ch24）用于预测浆/SAP平衡回潮|
|Ch25|p.627|数据|Monolayer a_w window|Above the monolayer (a_w, 0.32–0.43) for onion, increasing moisture contents resulted in greater reaction rates|★★★｜数值为洋葱专用，框架可用于求浆/SAP单层a_w|
|Ch25|p.627|数据|Carotenoid optimum a_w|The maximum stability was near 0.43 a_w (corresponding to an equilibrium moisture content of 8.8–10%)|★★｜食品色素专属数值，不可移植|
|Ch25|p.627|定义|Glass transition Tg|Glass transition is a second-order phase transition that occurs over the temperature range at which amorphous solid materials are transformed into viscous, liquid state|★★★★★｜无定形聚合物Tg决定坍塌/结块/发黏，直连卫材涂层与SAP|
|Ch25|p.627|论断|Microbiological stability a_w<0.6|for complete microbiological stability, water activity of the system should be below 0.6.|★★★★★｜完整微生物稳定a_w<0.6，可作浆/SAP内控参考|
|Ch25|p.627|案例|Tg-sh shrinkage protection|infusion of sugar during osmotic dehydration ... brought about some protection against shrinkage ... 20–65% increase in volume|★★★｜增塑溶质保护收缩，类比添加剂的Tg调控|
|Ch25|p.628|数据|Blanching microbial reduction|Reduction in total count during blanching was found to be greater than 99.9%.|★★★｜预处理灭酶/降菌>99.9%，类比原料卫生前处理|
|Ch25|p.629|定义|BET monolayer optimum|the optimal amount of water for long-term storage corresponds in most dehydrated foods to the Brunauer–Emmett–Teller (BET) monolayer value.|★★★★★｜最优残水=单层值，反对盲目求最干|
|Ch25|p.620|论断|Rate-quality trade-off|In general, rapid drying retained a greater amount of ascorbic acid than slow drying.|★★★★｜速率-品质权衡：快干保热敏但增收缩，映射热敏层干燥|
|Ch25|p.624|案例|Freeze-dried flavor retention|In general, freeze-dried products had more preferable flavors than air-dried ones except in the case of onions, for which an air-dried product had a stronger flavor due to entrapment of volatile oils by shrinkage.|★★★｜冻干保挥发/热敏物性，热风因收缩截留，类比干燥方式选择|
|Ch25|p.621|论断|Sulfite protective effect|Sulfur dioxide was found to have a pronounced protective effect on carotenoids of unblanched carrots during dehydration|★★｜亚硫酸盐护色为食品添加剂机制，卫材无直接对应|
|Ch25|p.623|定义|Nonenzymatic browning NEB|a group of diverse reactions between amino groups and active carbonyl groups, leading eventually to the formation of insoluble, brown, polymeric pigments, collectively known as melanoidin pigments.|★★｜美拉德为食品专属，但其"氨基-羰基聚合生色"机理可类比聚合物黄变|
|Ch25|p.627|数据|Shelf-life model a_w T|Actual (and Predicted) Shelf Life of Dried Onion Flakes ... at a_w 0.32 >631 days at 20C vs 59 days at 40C|★★★★｜a_w与温度联合预测货架期，方法可迁移至浆/SAP储存模型|
|Ch26|p.633|数据|草药市场规模|The combined sales of herbal medicine products in major markets around the world exceeded US$12 billion in 1994|★｜仅为行业背景，说明干燥品质经济价值，对卫材无直接技术含义|
|Ch26|p.634|论断|品质取决于活性成分保留|The quality of the herbs depends very much on the contents of active ingredients. It is known that heating or thermal drying, if not carried out properly, can cause a significant loss of the active ingredients.|★★★｜确立'干燥品质=功能载荷存活率'范式，可移植为绒毛浆/SAP以功能指标(CRC、残单)而非仅含水率验收|
|Ch26|p.634|论断|温度双侧窗口机理|The selection of proper drying temperature is vital. Too high a temperature may cause loss of active ingredients. On the other hand, too low a temperature may actually accelerate decomposition by promoting enzyme activity within the plant itself or even microbial attack, particularly for sugar-containing substances.|★★★｜最具移植性的机理：低温延长湿态停留反而加速降解，卫材需设上下双边界(浆板霉变/SAP残单副反应)|
|Ch26|p.634|数据|叶花干燥失重85%|Leaves and flowers usually lose up to 85% of their weight on drying.|★｜植物基材初含水极高，与卫材干态基材无可比性|
|Ch26|p.634|论断|根茎比叶花难干|Tubers and roots take longer to dry than flowers and leaves even though the former are often cut up into pieces.|★★｜提示几何切分不能完全消除内部扩散阻力，类比SAP颗粒粒径与干燥时间的非线性关系|
|Ch26|p.634|论断|热泵干燥保色保活|This type of dryer can work at low air temperature; thus color and active substances remain intact. Another feature of the heat pump dryer is its characteristic low energy consumption|★★｜低温热泵兼顾品质与能耗，可作为卫材热敏涂层/乳液固化的低温干燥候选路线|
|Ch26|p.634|论断|冻干仅适高值低量|Freeze drying is an expensive process recommended only for high-value, low-volume products.|★★｜明确冻干经济边界，卫材大宗基材(浆/无纺布)不适用，仅限高值功能样品|
|Ch26|p.635|论断|三类茶需不同干燥系统|The majority of the teas can be classified as green tea, oolong tea, and black tea. Each requires a different process and hence a different drying system is required. The quality of dried tea depends to a great extent on the drying system and operating conditions used.|★★｜同一原料经不同热历史得到不同产品，说明干燥是组分调控手段而非单纯脱水——方法论层面可借鉴|
|Ch26|p.635|数据|绿茶初含水与杀青温度|The initial water content of fresh tea leaves after plucking is around 75–78% (wet basis) in spring and 65–70% (wet basis) in autumn|★｜食品基材初含水数据，与卫材无关|
|Ch26|p.635|论断|杀青灭酶阻止氧化|This process is designed to arrest the enzymatic reaction, oxidation in particular.|★★｜'高温灭活以锁定状态'思路，类比无纺布热定型/SAP表面交联的不可逆固化步骤|
|Ch26|p.635|数据|绿茶多段降温干燥程序|the wok temperature at 150–160°C and drying time of 30–40 min until moisture content reaches about 20%. Afterwards, the wok surface temperature is reduced to 80–100°C and drying continues for 60–90 min to have the moisture content of 9–10%|★★｜典型递降温阶梯程序实例，验证'前段高温后段降温'的通用干燥策略|
|Ch26|p.635|论断|回软防边缘焦脆|It is recommended to have the dried tea leaves tempered to prevent the edges of leaves from ''crisping.''|★★｜中间回软(tempering)平衡内部水分梯度以防边缘过干脆化，对应卷材边缘卷曲/脆化缺陷的抑制思路|
|Ch26|p.637|论断|高温封锁风味并终止发酵|The higher temperature step is necessary to stop the enzymatic fermentation reaction and seal the flavors inside the leaves.|★★｜末段高温作为'终止+封锁'工序的功能定位，可类比湿巾涂层最终固化段|
|Ch26|p.638|论断|振动流化床减少碎料|the grid can be vibrated with half-amplitude of several millimeters and frequencies in the range of 10–20 Hz using metal springs to make a vibro-fluidized bed dryer... It is claimed to produce less fines and result in gentler handling of the fragile leaves resulting in a better quality product.|★★★｜振动流化(10–20 Hz、半振幅数毫米)可降低脆性颗粒产尘，直接对应SAP流化干燥的细粉/粉尘控制|
|Ch26|p.638|论断|流化床首区最大风量|In the first zone of the dryer, the air flow rate is the maximum to remove the very high moisture content of the fermented leaf rapidly.|★★｜分区风量递减匹配含水递降，是SAP多区流化床风量分配的通用原则|
|Ch26|p.639|论断|棚式干燥上层偏干需换盘|In a shed-type dryer the moisture laden, cooler air will sink to the lower levels of the room. Thus, the upper trays will tend to dry faster. To maintain a uniform drying, either the upper trays are loaded with large roots or the trays are rebuilt during the course of drying|★★｜自然对流分层导致批内不均，提示卫材静态批式干燥需换位或强制均流以控批内离散|
|Ch26|p.639|论断|末段不可提温防褐变|The danger of brown roots developing is greater toward the end of drying. It is not recommended to accelerate the drying by increasing the drying temperature at the last stage of drying.|★★★｜降率期蒸发冷却减弱、物料温度飙升，末段严禁提温——与Ch15一致，是卫材防表面硬化/焦化的硬规则|
|Ch26|p.641|论断|阶梯干燥兼顾速率与低温|In order to maintain a high drying rate and a low temperature of herb pieces, it is suggested to use staged drying with variation of temperatures of heated air.|★★★｜核心方法论：温度程序化替代单一设定点，解决速率-品质冲突，可直接移植到SAP/浆板干燥|
|Ch26|p.641|数据|甘草四工况速率-温度对照|in drying of 400 g of Radix Glycyrrhizae from 0.44 to 0.08 kg water/kg solids, it took 70, 61, 48, and 55 min, respectively, at temperatures of 40, 50, 70, and (30→77→60)°C|★★★｜阶梯工况以55 min接近70℃恒温的48 min，而物料最高温仅49℃(对70℃工况63℃)，是速率-热应力解耦的定量证据|
|Ch26|p.641|数据|床深倍增使时间2.7倍且ΔT扩大|When the bed depth is increased from 25 to 50 mm, the drying time is 2.7 times longer and within the material, the maximum temperature differences increased from 10 to 18°C at a drying temperature of 66°C. The optimal depth for the experimental facilities described previously is about 30 mm.|★★★｜床深→内部温差→时间的定量耦合，直接指导SAP流化床/静态床料层厚度选择以控批内均匀性|
|Ch26|p.641|论断|风量与初含水不影响品质|More experiments by these authors show that neither the air flowrate nor the initial moisture content affect the quality of dried herb pieces. The quality of the dried herbs depends on the drying technology and drying conditions with temperature being a key parameter to control.|★★｜论断温度为主控参数、风量次要——但与床深效应及挥发性成分汽提存在张力，卫材移植前须自行验证|
|Ch26|p.641|论断|允许温度值可能过高|It is anticipated that for some herbs the currently listed values of allowed temperature are simply too high.|★★｜作者自我质疑既有工艺上限，提示卫材不应盲信供应商给定的干燥温度上限，须以功能指标复核|
|Ch26|p.641|论断|EHD低温高品质但经济性未明|Because this drying technique requires only slightly heated air for drying, it gives very high quality of dried material. It is claimed as high quality as freeze dried materials are obtained... The economics of this novel drying process are not clear as yet.|★★｜EHD为低温高品质路线但经济性未验证，卫材大宗应用尚不具备可行性|
|Ch26|p.641|实验|红外干燥活性损失更高|When infrared drying is used, the loss of active ingredients is even higher for some herbs as shown in Table 26.4|★★｜红外因表面过热对部分热敏组分更不利，卫材涂层固化选红外前须做实测比对|
|Ch26|p.642|论断|供热与排湿方式影响品质|Besides the material temperature, the way in which the heat is supplied and the moisture is taken away from the drying chamber definitely plays a role in the quality of the dried herbs.|★★★｜供热模式与排湿路径独立于温度影响品质，卫材选热风/红外/穿透干燥须按损伤机理而非速率选型|
|Ch26|p.642|数据|红外+振动流化协同改善|When infrared drying is combined with vibro-fluidized bed, Figure 26.10, the drying rate as well as the quality of the dried product is improved significantly.|★★★｜颗粒运动消除红外表面过热惩罚，是SAP等热敏颗粒干燥的推荐组合(红外+振动流化)|
|Ch26|p.643|数据|薄荷与苔草活性100%损失|4 HerbaMenthae 68.5 75 100 100|★★｜挥发性成分(薄荷醇)在两种方式下均100%损失，说明汽提损失与热分解机理不同——卫材香精/功能助剂干燥须区分两种流失路径|
|Ch26|p.643|论断|均匀干燥提升品质|Uniform drying of the small herb pieces can give better quality of the products.|★★★｜'均匀性即品质'的收束结论，对应卫材批内/幅向水分均匀性是核心质量指标|
|Ch27|p.646|论断|去皮决定成品率与废料|Peeling is one of the most important steps in potato processing. The yield of finished product depends on effectiveness, efficiency, and method of peeling.|★★☆☆☆｜前处理(去皮/解纤)决定可用产量与废料，类比绒毛浆前端解纤工段对得率与废料的支配)|
|Ch27|p.648|论断|热烫钝化酶保色|Blanching destroys or inactivates enzymes; otherwise, potatoes may darken during dehydration and develop off-flavors and off-odors during storage.|★★★☆☆｜前处理钝化酶/稳定基团是货架色稳的首道杠杆，类比卫材添加剂上游预稳定)|
|Ch27|p.648|论断|亚硫酸盐防非酶褐变|Sulfite protects the product from nonenzymatic browning or scorching during dehydration and increases the storage life of the product under adverse temperature conditions.|★★★☆☆｜化学保护剂解耦'耐干燥温度'与'货架保色'，正是湿巾涂层面临的同一解耦问题)|
|Ch27|p.650|数据|两段式切片干燥温度|The temperature of the drying air in the first section of the first stage is in the range 93–127°C, in the second section of first stage 71–105°C, and in the second stage 55–82°C.|★★★☆☆｜一段高温表面脱水+后续降温缓干的结合曲线，可直接借鉴为热敏网材的分段温控模板)|
|Ch27|p.650|论断|长期储存需3-4%wb吸干|A further extension of the storage time will require reduction of moisture content to 3–4% (wb), which can be achieved by long-time sorption drying using sorption agents such as calcium oxide.|★★★☆☆｜对流干燥只到6-7%wb，终段须靠吸附/低湿调理达存储水分底，类比卫材芯体的终段低湿调理区)|
|Ch27|p.649|公式|水分扩散率D(m,T)参比模型|D(m,T) = a₀ exp( a₁(1/m − 1/m₀) − a₂(1/T − 1/T₀) )|★★★☆☆｜扩散率随水分降而降=降率期的形式表达，是任何内部扩散受限干燥的通用建模语汇)|
|Ch27|p.649|公式|D(m,T)乘积指数型|D(m,T) = a₀ exp(a₁ m) exp(−a₂(T−T₃))|★★☆☆☆｜另一参比形式，常数属食品专用，仅函数形式可迁移)|
|Ch27|p.649|公式|D(m,T,L)厚度线性型|D(m,T,L) = a₀ + a₁ m + a₂ T + a₃ L|★★☆☆☆｜引入厚度L的线性扩散模型，提示片厚对干燥动力学的影响)|
|Ch27|p.649|数据|活化能40-52 kJ/mol|Moisture Diffusivity and Activation Energy... 40–52 kJ/mol range for potato air-drying|★★☆☆☆｜土豆空气干燥的活化能量级，仅作量级参考，不可套用于绒毛/SAP)|
|Ch27|p.649|数据|BET/GAB单分子层|BET and GAB Monolayer Values... M₀ ≈ 0.029–0.083 kg/kg db for potato|★★★☆☆｜单分子层水分是品质稳定存储阈值，类比卫材芯体的平衡水分/RH稳定性概念)|
|Ch27|p.654|实验|渗透脱水作前处理|a solution of 50% sugar and 10% salt at 47°C for 4 h was optimum for osmotic preconcentration. The moisture content of potato cubes was reduced from 84 to 60% (wb).|★★★☆☆｜渗透脱水在热干燥前先抽走约30%水、降干燥负荷，机制家族同Ch28，详见Ch28)|
|Ch27|p.655|论断|方波温度降色变87%|The percentage reduction in overall color change for potato was about 87% (square waveform 35°C, amplitude 5°C, peak to valley 10°C, cycle time 60 min).|★★★☆☆｜振荡式温度程序比固定设定点更能抑制降解通路，是通用的质量控制策略)|
|Ch27|p.655|论断|微波仅用于终段干燥|Microwave drying has been evaluated for final stages of drying (between 30 and 6%) of different potato products.|★★★☆☆｜体积加热保留给结合水尾部，避免表面过热，是分周期选能的模式)|
|Ch27|p.656|论断|低温干燥收缩更大|The degree of shrinkage at a low drying temperature (40°C) was greater than at high temperature (70°C).|★★★☆☆｜收缩由停留时间积分决定而非峰值温度，对尺寸敏感非织造网材具借鉴)|
|Ch27|p.656|实验|孔隙率先降后升|internal porosity of potato cylinders first decreased with drying time then it increased up to 10% at the end of drying. The volume shrinkage exhibited a linear correlation with respect to moisture content.|★★☆☆☆｜孔隙率/收缩与水分的耦合演变，是结构形成机理的参考)|
|Ch27|p.656|数据|营养保留表27.12|thiamine collapses to 5% (raw→granule), ascorbic acid to 29–31%... starch and protein largely retained|★★★☆☆｜水溶性热敏微量营养素是最脆弱类别，类比功能湿巾中活性成分的脆弱性)|
|Ch27|p.653|论断|淀粉糊化温度约束|moist starch granules begin to swell (gelatinize) at 45°C... temperatures of moist starch are maintained below 35°C during drying|★☆☆☆☆｜食品专用约束(淀粉糊化)，无聚合物网材类比，列作局限)|
|Ch27|p.655|论断|品质受热处理敏感|Potato is sensitive to heat and mechanical stress; thus thermal degradation of potato products leads to losses in quality (color, structure, shape, etc.) as well as nutrient content.|★★★☆☆｜热/机械应力致品质损失的总论断，与卫材热敏网材同源)|
|Ch27|p.660|数据|人参皂苷对温度极敏感|The ginsenoside content may be reduced by over 25% by drying the root using 44°C air instead of 38°C.|★★★★☆｜6°C超出即损失>25%活性，是两温窗论点最有力的量化数据点，直接支撑载活湿巾温控)|
|Ch27|p.660|数据|人参初水分与终水分|The initial moisture content of ginseng is about 70–80% wet basis. The optimal final moisture content for storage is about 12–13% wet basis... air temperatures of 32 to 38°C.|★★★☆☆｜热敏活性物须低温(32-38°C)长时干燥至12-13%wb，提供低温干燥窗口参考)|
|Ch27|p.657|论断|蘑菇变温干燥保色|Unblanched mushroom slices dried at 40–50°C result in light-colored products. Light-colored dried mushroom can also be obtained by drying at 40–45°C at the beginning and then raising the temperature gradually to about 60–70°C for 10 to 12 h.|★★☆☆☆｜先低后高变温保色的又一案例，支撑温度程序化策略)|
|Ch27|p.661|论断|真空/冻干质优但贵|Although product quality obtained by vacuum or freeze-drying is superior, both methods are expensive and they are not used on industrial scale for potato products.|★★☆☆☆｜高品质干燥方式成本权衡的通用结论)|
|Ch27|p.659|论断|花生缓干防裂|the moisture removal rate is maintained at 0.5% per hour. Peanuts dried too rapidly or stored at low moisture content will have a high percentage of split and bald kernels|★★☆☆☆｜降率(0.5%/h)防裂/防缺陷，是'过快干燥致缺陷'的又一佐证)|
|Ch27|p.661|论断|微波终干已工业化|Microwave finish drying of potato chips has found practical industrial application [14].|★★★☆☆｜微波终段干燥的工业化先例，支撑期末段体积加热思路)|
|Ch28|p.663|论断|渗透脱水无相变除水|Water, on the other hand, is removed by osmosis and the cell sap is concentrated without a phase transition of the solvent.|★★★★★｜水以液态而非汽化被移除，正是SAP以零汽化潜热直接吸液的核心优势)|
|Ch28|p.664|公式|水化学势Eq28.1|mu_w = mu^0_w + R T ln a_w|★★★★★｜溶剂化学势表达式，正是Flory-Huggins聚合物溶液热力学与SAP溶胀平衡所用的同一式)|
|Ch28|p.664|公式|渗透压Eq28.2|Pi V_w = R T ln a|★★★★★｜渗透压=跨半透膜的化学势差，正是SAP交联网络溶胀压的同一物理量)|
|Ch28|p.664|公式|渗透压Eq28.3|Pi = 4.6063x10^5 T ln a_w|★★★★★｜渗透压显式定律，可直接作为SAP吸液驱动力起点)|
|Ch28|p.664|论断|电解质渗透压更高(唐南萌芽)|Electrolytes show higher osmotic pressure than nonelectrolytes because each ion affects the chemical potential of a solvent.|★★★★★｜'每个离子都影响溶剂化学势'即唐南平衡定性表述，是SAP在盐尿中坍缩的根本机理)|
|Ch28|p.664|数据|微生物抑制阈值|Most bacteria, yeasts, and moulds do not proliferate at P > 12.7 MPa, P > 17.3 MPa, and P > 30.1 MPa, respectively.|★★★☆☆｜渗透压抑微生物的定量阈值，与高渗盐水防腐的卫材关联)|
|Ch28|p.665|定义|半透膜质膜|The plasmalemma is a protein–lipid layer... permeable to water, and selectively permeable to other substances.|★★★★★｜'透水不透溶质'的半透膜定义，正是SAP交联网络作为半透基质的结构角色)|
|Ch28|p.665|定义|膨压|The difference between the osmotic pressure in the cell and in its surroundings is called the turgor pressure.|★★★★☆｜内部渗透压与外部之差=膨压，类比SAP凝胶相相对外界的溶胀压)|
|Ch28|p.666|定义|质壁分离=消溶胀|A cell placed in a hypertonic solution... will lose water... detachment of plasmalemma from the cell wall. This process is called plasmolysis.|★★★★★｜高外渗压致内腔坍缩，是SAP在盐水/载荷下消溶胀的机械类比)|
|Ch28|p.667|论断|逆对流:水出溶质入|the water flux will predominate over the osmoactive substance flux. This is due to osmotic pressure flow... When plasmolysis occurs... the flux of osmoactive substance will be equal to or will surpass the flux of water.|★★★★★｜水出/溶质入的逆对流通量，正是SAP吸液(逆网络弹性阻力)与盐渗出的同一现象)|
|Ch28|p.667|图表|图28.6双流通量|countercurrent fluxes of water and the osmoactive substance occur (Figure 28.6)|★★★★☆｜同时发生的水与溶质逆对流通量图，对应SAP吸液与离子交换的耦合)|
|Ch28|p.669|论断|膜/网络是主要传质阻力|the cell membrane (plasmalemma) represents the major resistance to mass transfer... When the thickness of the sample increases, the extracellular space may become the limiting factor.|★★★★★｜半透边界而非本体决定速率，恰如SAP交联网络弹性模量是溶胀速率/容量的限制阻力)|
|Ch28|p.669|论断|膜非必需,基质扩散即可|membranes are not necessary for osmotic dehydration and merely diffusive properties of the material are responsible for high water flux|★★★★☆｜无需膜、基质本身扩散即足够，强化'交联网络自身即半透元件')|
|Ch28|p.670|论断|厚度不宜超10mm|Lenart and Lewicki have shown that the thickness of the material should not exceed 10 mm|★★★☆☆｜薄层高比表面积利于边界控制传输，指导SAP层/气流成网薄层设计)|
|Ch28|p.670|论断|人造半透膜降溶质渗入|Artificial semipermeable membranes are formed from pectins or starch, alginate or low-methoxyl pectin... Coatings significantly reduce solute incorporation into tissue and result in increased weight loss|★★★★★｜涂层降溶质渗入保水通量，类比SAP表面交联/包覆调控吸速与渗液)|
|Ch28|p.672|数据|最佳水损:固渗比在20-40C|a temperature between 20°C and 40°C is recommended|★★★☆☆｜20-40°C为水损/固渗比最优窗口，指导温敏脱水的低温段选择)|
|Ch28|p.672|数据|温度升促溶质渗入|Increased temperature promotes penetration of osmoactive substance into the tissue|★★★☆☆｜升温同时增溶质渗入，类比升温对SAP离子交换/渗漏的促进)|
|Ch28|p.674|论断|无相变节能|Osmotic dehydration is distinctive in that water is removed from the product without undergoing the phase change. It offers a considerable potential for energy saving|★★★★★｜免汽化相变=节能根源，与SAP吸液零干燥能耗同源)|
|Ch28|p.675|数据|能耗对比|convection drying needs some 5 MJ/kg of evaporated water, which is at least twice as much as is needed in osmotic dehydration|★★★★★｜对流干燥~5MJ/kg vs 渗透脱水100-2400kJ/kg，量化免相变节能，支撑SAP吸液零能耗论点)|
|Ch28|p.678|论断|OD非保存而是前处理|osmotic dehydration cannot be treated as a food preservation process per se. It is a pretreatment that removes a certain amount of water|★★★★☆｜OD是降载前处理(如Ch27土豆)，类比SAP前段液态脱水减负)|
|Ch28|p.678|数据|渗透法更强降结合水|Osmotic dehydration done for 0.5 h led to a sixfold decrease of water-binding forces at the surface|★★★☆☆｜渗透除水对水结合力影响远大于对流干燥，关乎芯体持水结构)|
|Ch28|p.679|论断|OD+对流品质更优|shrinkage is much smaller when compared with that observed in convection-dried products at the same water activity... natural color, well-preserved flavor, and high retention of vitamins|★★★☆☆｜OD联合干燥 shrinkage小、保留好，支撑前处理提质思路)|
|Ch28|p.679|论断|OD免二氧化硫防腐|Use of osmotic dehydration practically eliminates the need to use preservatives such as sulfur dioxide in fruits.|★★★☆☆｜免防腐剂的稳定化思路，类比卫材减敏/无防腐方向)|
|Ch28|p.669|实验|电场脉冲指数增扩散|diffusion coefficient for water and solute increased exponentially with electric field strength|★★☆☆☆｜预处理指数增渗的机理案例，可作SAP基质改性(电/高压)的启发)|
|Ch29|p.687|论断|质量不可妥协零污染|The pharmaceutical industry is one in which quality of the final product cannot be compromised. Any deterioration of the product (e.g., by microbial infection, oxidation, thermal decomposition, contamination by metallic particles or by unremoved organic solvent) must be avoided at any cost.|★★★★｜医疗级无纺布/药用湿巾须同等零容忍"生物负载、氧化、金属、溶剂残留控制姿态")|
|Ch29|p.687|论断|GMP结构材料与闭式循环|Noncontaminating dryer construction materials are used, like polished stainless steel or enameled iron. Closed-cycle dryers are often required as moisture removed is often an organic solvent... Drying must be often performed in inert gas to avoid oxidation or explosion if solvent is flammable.|★★★★｜抛光不锈钢/搪瓷结构+闭式循环+惰性气体，对应医用敷料线结构材质与溶剂安全规范)|
|Ch29|p.688|论断|能耗在高价值品中次要|Since most pharmaceuticals are low-tonnage and high-value products, considerations of energy saving are usually of secondary importance in the selection of the drying process.,卫材相关性★★★｜高价值药用活性(药用湿巾)质量优先于kWh/kg，可作为优质干燥设备capex论证)
Ch29,p.688,定义,按孔径/水-材键分四类",Sazhin has developed a classification based in principle on Rebinder's proposal, which classifies all materials into four groups. The quantitative basis of the classification is the minimum pore diameter from which moisture is to be removed during drying.|★★★★｜按最小脱湿孔径将材料分为四组，对应自由水→毛细管→吸附→化学结合的水-材键强度阶梯，适用于芯体水分去除层级)|
|Ch29|p.689|数据|四组孔径与干燥时间|Group I: >100 nm, 0.5–3.0 s; Group II: 100–6 nm, 3–30 s; Group III: 6–4 nm, 0.5–2 min; Group IV: ultramicropores, 10–60 min... particle size >90 min.|★★★｜孔径越小干燥越慢、所需设备越苛刻，类比SAP网络/纤维结合水脱除难度排序)|
|Ch29|p.689|论断|超细粉不宜流化床|very fine powders and ultrafine powders are not recommended for drying in fluid bed dryers owing to their very low entrainment velocities and often poor fluidization quality due to agglomeration.,卫材相关性★★★｜超细粉(类SAP细粉)流化床易夹带/团聚，选型须谨慎，对应SAP细粉干燥工艺)
Ch29,p.690,论断,升温变黏致去流化",Certain products not sticky at normal temperatures become sticky at elevated temperatures due to plasticizing or dissolving in moisture. This may lead to defluidization of a bed at a certain temperature.,卫材相关性★★★｜残留水分使细粉升温塑化变黏、流化床失流化，警示SAP细粉/添加剂粉末干燥温度窗口)
Ch29,p.691,公式,热分解一级/Arrhenius",thermal decomposition follows a first-order reaction kinetics, that is, dc/dt = -k c, where c is the actual concentration, c0 is the initial concentration, k is the rate constant (1/s)... k = A e^(-E/RT)... thiamine hydrochloride... E = 92.18 kJ/mol and A = 9.58*10^10 1/min.,卫材相关性★★★★｜热分解c/c0一级动力学+Arrhenius，可照搬评估湿巾活性物干燥热损失窗口)
Ch29,p.691,论断,仅脱附等温线用于干燥",For drying purposes only desorption isotherms are of practical significance.,卫材相关性★★★★｜干燥只取脱附(而非吸附)等温线，是医用湿巾终水分与回渗水分状态设定的正确基准)
Ch29,p.692,论断,胶体体强滞后(淀粉/明胶)",Materials with intensive hysteresis... belong to colloidal bodies according to Rebinder's classification. Most of the final pharmaceutical products belong to this group. All tableting formulations containing starch or gelatin are obtained by spray drying a mixture... which are colloid in nature.,卫材相关性★★★｜淀粉/明胶等胶体强滞后，类比SAP/多糖类吸水胶体的吸附-脱附滞后行为)
Ch29,p.693,数据,气动干燥高蒸发率仅除非结合水",High evaporation rates (values as high as 240–1200 kg moisture per h and per m2 of contact area are generally met) and cocurrent contact protect the particles from overheating. Very short times of contact allow drying only the unbound moisture from small particles.,卫材相关性★★★｜气动干燥240–1200 kg/m²·h仅除小颗粒非结合水，对应喷射/气动干燥只去表面水的边界)
Ch29,p.697,论断,真空干燥适热敏/易氧化",Materials that are thermolabile or easily oxidizable, especially when wet, can be dried in vacuum dryers.,卫材相关性★★★｜热敏/易氧化物料用真空干燥，类比含易氧化活性物的湿巾低温干燥)
Ch29,p.699,数据,冻干形成易再水化多孔结构",After all moisture sublimes, a very fine porous structure remains that can be easily rehydrated. It was noticed that solutions diluted below 1% (weight) concentration of solids do not produce such structure. The structure is labile and can collapse if its temperature is raised above a certain temperature of collapse.,卫材相关性★★★｜冻干产物为无定形多孔、易再水化但高于坍塌温度即崩塌，类比湿巾中冻干/喷雾干燥无定形活性物的稳定性)
Ch29,p.700,数据,坍塌温度(玻璃化类比)",This temperature can be as low as -40°C (e.g., for glucose and fructose) or -10°C (e.g., for starches and proteins) and increases with the increase of molecular weight of the lattice and its dryness.,卫材相关性★★★★｜坍塌温度即无定形固体玻璃化转变(Tg)类比，是多糖类/蛋白类活性物干燥与储运稳定性上限)
Ch29,p.702,论断,湿法制粒液桥结晶成固桥",For wet granulation, a suitable liquid is sprayed onto the vigorously mixed powder. Adhesion of particles takes place owing to the development of liquid bridges and capillary forces between particles. In the drying phase following the granulation process, solid bridges develop owing to crystallization of dissolved substances.,卫材相关性★★★｜湿法制粒液桥→毛细力→干燥结晶固桥，类比SAP/纤维湿团聚与干燥后结块机理)
Ch29,p.704,论断,HEPA与百级洁净",High-efficiency particulate air (HEPA) filters... claimed to remove up to 99.97% of all particles larger than 0.3 µm... class 100 air cleanliness according to U.S. Federal Standard 209A must be maintained.,卫材相关性★★★★｜HEPA 99.97%@>0.3µm + 百级层流，是医疗级无纺布洁净转化区的直接规范)
Ch29,p.707,论断,灭菌与无金属污染设计",most aseptic dryers are sterilized at temperatures in the range 200 to 250°C for several hours... All locations where mechanical friction of parts take place should be eliminated as they can produce small metallic contaminations... Rotating or sliding parts should be avoided.,卫材相关性★★★★｜200–250°C灭菌、消除滑动/旋转件防金属污染，是医用线设备设计准则)
Ch29,p.708,论断,闭式循环惰性气防爆",A large portion of all pharmaceuticals is obtained from nonwater solutions... These solvents... may not be disposed off into atmosphere even at very low concentrations... |Ch30|p.711|定义|纳米材料尺度定义|Nanomaterials can be clusters of atoms, grains 100 nm in size, fibers that are less than 100 nm in diameter, films that are less than 100 nm in thickness, nanoholes, and composites... Materials in nanosize range exhibit fundamentally new properties.|★★★｜纳米尺度(<100nm)材料呈现新特性，对应SAP细粉与纳米纤维素的尺度效应与特殊聚集行为)|
|Ch30|p.712|论断|干燥中保持分散是硬要求|Nanoparticles tend to agglomerate and properties of nanoparticles are adversely affected if we do not choose appropriate drying methods. |Ch30|p.713|论断|蒸发三段与临界点|solvent evaporation involves three periods, namely a constant rate drying, a first falling rate, and a second falling rate... At the critical point... the radius of curved liquid is equal to the size of the gel pores... capillary tension reaches maximal value.,卫材相关性★★★★｜恒率→降率转变的临界点毛细张力最大，对应湿巾/芯体干燥中应力峰与微裂风险)
Ch30,p.714,公式,毛细压力主因方程(30.1)",Capillary pressure is the major cause leading to cracking of the gel skeletons or agglomeration of nanoparticles during drying: P = 2σ cosθ / r_p. where P is the capillary pressure, s is the liquid–vapor interfacial energy (or surface tension), u is the contact angle, and r is the radius of curvature.,卫材相关性★★★★★｜P=2σcosθ/r_p 是裂纹与团聚的共同根源，SAP细粉/纳米纤维素因r_p极小而毛细压力极大，须据此控团聚)
Ch30,p.714,论断,降毛细压三杠杆",in order to prevent nanoparticles from agglomerating or gels from collapsing, capillary pressure must be decreased to a minimum. The following means can decrease capillary pressure: Reducing surface tension... Changing wetting angle and making it approach 90° (surface modification)... Enlarging pore size of gels properly... and making it uniformly.,卫材相关性★★★★★｜降表面张力σ、接触角θ→90°、增大且均匀化孔径r_p 三杠杆，正是SAP细粉/纳米纤维素低温防结块的可操作手段)
Ch30,p.715,论断,硬团聚的化学键根源",chemical bond theory: nonbridging hydroxyls existing on a nanoparticle surface are the basic sources of hard agglomerates. The reaction of nonbridging hydroxyls on the surface of neighboring particles... Me–O–Me group accounts for the formation of agglomerates.|★★★★｜表面非桥联羟基缩合为Me-O-Me是硬团聚"不可逆根源，决定SAP/纳米纤维素团聚能否被剪切再分散)|
|Ch30|p.715|论断|团聚机理无统一定论|At present, there are no generally accepted views on the formation of hard agglomerates of nanoparticles. There are several representative theories proposed, e.g., including crystal bridge theory, capillary pressure theory, hydrogen bond theory, chemical bond theory.,卫材相关性★★★｜硬团聚四机理并存、无定论，提示卫材须实测哪种键主导，而非套用单一模型)
Ch30,p.716,论断,冷冻干燥特点(水膨胀分隔)",Water undergoes some volume expansion when freezing, which can separate adjacent particles when water turns into ice. Nanoparticles are prevented from agglomerating due to the formation of the solid phase... high-energy vapor–liquid interfaces are replaced by low-energy vapor–solid interface.,卫材相关性★★★★｜冷冻时水膨胀与固-气界面替代液-气界面可抑制纳米颗粒团聚，类比含纳米纤维素凝胶的温和干燥保孔)
Ch30,p.717,数据,超临界干燥消除毛细压",Supercritical drying prevents capillary pressure between the vapor–liquid interface and the solid part of gel... carbon dioxide has the low critical parameters (Tc 31°C, Pc 7.29 MPa), but the critical pressure for water is as high as 21.77 MPa.,卫材相关性★★★｜超临界(CO2 Tc31°C/Pc7.29MPa)消除气-液界面毛细压，是零毛细应力干燥的标杆方法)
Ch30,p.717,数据,煅烧升温粒子长大",TiO2 particles size: 22.1 nm @450°C → 23.9 @500 → 34.4 @600 → 41.1 @700 → 46.9 @800 → 49.6 nm @900°C.,卫材相关性★★★｜煅烧温度升高粒子显著长大，提示热处理后纳米尺度不可逆粗化，对含纳米填料的卫材热处理须控温)
Ch30,p.718,论断,溶剂置换降团聚",Replacing water with selected solvents having low surface tension can yield products with minor agglomeration... The functional groups of organic molecules replace nonbridging hydroxyls partially as well as produce sterically hindered effects to prevent agglomeration.,卫材相关性★★★★★｜用水置换为低表面张力有机溶剂可大幅降团聚(有机基团取代非桥联羟基+位阻)，直接指导纳米纤维素/CMC凝胶脱水)
Ch30,p.722,数据,溶剂置换超临界最优(TiO2)",CP1 Direct oven drying calcining 50–100 nm / 4.88 m2/g / 0.027; CP2 Ethanol-replacement supercritical 10–20 nm / 113.8 m2/g / 0.41; CP3 Ethanol-replacement oven 20–40 nm / 60.7 m2/g / 0.34.|★★★★★｜同配方不同干燥法使粒径差5倍、比表面积差23倍，量化证明干燥路径"而非配方决定纳米结构)|

Ch30,p.723,数据,超临界MgO最小最完整",sample 7 (Alcohol Supercritical drying 60°C) has the smallest particle size (18 nm) and perfect crystallinity; mild temperature (60°C) and free of capillary pressure during supercritical drying are responsible for this.,卫材相关性★★★★｜无毛细压的低温超临界得到最小最完整晶体，佐证低温+零毛细应力对纳米结构保存的关键性)
Ch30,p.725,论断,团聚仍是主挑战+安全",Agglomeration remains a major challenge. nanoparticles are difficult to collect if emitted in air. |Ch31|p.727|论断|陶瓷慢干防裂保形|drying is usually carried out slowly in order to avoid undesirable cracks and deformations. The reason is often attributed to the fact that ceramic drying involves maintaining a molded configuration.|★★★★｜为保形须慢干防裂，正是非织造/纸巾线控制卷曲与开裂的同一约束：速率受形状保持而非产能主导)|
|Ch31|p.728|论断|四难之首:初始速率须受限|the rise in the drying rate must be naturally limited because the faster rate in the beginning of drying results in rapid attainment of the critical moisture content and then the remarkable reduction of the rate in the falling rate period,卫材相关性★★★★★｜恒率期速率须受限、过快则快速达临界水分后降率剧降，是表面硬化(恒率→降率)机制的操作表述)
Ch31,p.728,论断,收缩内应力致失效",failures and irregular deformation may be generated due to drying shrinkage and internal stress it induces,卫材相关性★★★★★｜干燥收缩与内应力致失效/变形，对应湿巾/芯体web的卷曲、开裂、起皱缺陷机理)

Ch31,p.730,论断,亚微米粒子呈渗透(非毛细)收缩",shrinkage due to drying generally arises particularly for materials including water by the osmotic suction potential within their internal voids as clay... the osmotic suction potential is brought about owing to an electrochemical effect between the water and the surface of particles smaller than micrometers or submicron order.,卫材相关性★★★★★｜亚微米颗粒(纤维素/纳米纤维素纤维)收缩由双电层/电化学渗透吸力驱动而非毛细，重框纤维web收缩机理)
Ch31,p.731,论断,水膜变薄粒子靠近收缩",The shrinkage of clay is affected by the reduced thickness of the water films formed on the particles due to the osmotic suction potential and particles approaching closer to one another as water is removed.,卫材相关性★★★★｜渗透吸力使颗粒间水膜变薄、粒子靠近而收缩，类比纤维间水膜排水导致的web收缩与密实)
Ch31,p.732,数据,液扩散主导至高水分",liquid diffusion dominates the movement until about 20%-dry basis in moisture content for stoneware clay and 30% for the mixture of 80% Kibushi clay and 20% feldspar.,卫材相关性★★★｜液扩散在降率前主导至高水分(石瓷20%、Kibushi混料30%db)，界定纤维web降率期起始的水分阈值)
Ch31,p.732,公式,有效扩散系数(卡曼k=5)",The effective diffusion coefficient D can be defined by D = a K_g^2 r_v^3 exp(K_w w) / (k_0 ...); Carman's constant k = 5; a=2.02*10^11 Pa, K=24.9 kg-solid/kg-water.,卫材相关性★★★｜有效湿扩散系数D含比表面积与水分指数项，可借为纤维web内部湿迁移的定量化起点)

|Ch32|p.742|论断|Storage microbiology threshold|To avoid significant microbiological degradation during storage, the material should be dried to a moisture content of 20 to 25% [2]|★★☆☆☆｜MC thresholds often set by biology not process; parallels hygiene-product storage|
|Ch32|p.743|论断|Drying not always economical|It is not obvious that a moist fuel must be dried before firing. On the contrary, this is considered uneconomical in many cases|★★★☆☆｜Core discipline: justify every drying step by threshold, not by reflex|
|Ch32|p.743|数据|Gasification/suspension MC limits|Gasification moisture should not exceed 10 to 15%; suspension firing requires less than 15% moisture|★☆☆☆☆｜Process-threshold logic transferable; numbers not|
|Ch32|p.744|论断|Flue-gas drying marginal returns|The primary improvement takes place when the exhaust temperature is lowered to 100 °C. Further drying leads to marginal savings only|★★★☆☆｜Energy cliff past threshold; directly informs bale-MC optimization|

|Ch32|p.747|数据|Steam-dryer electricity MVR|total electricity consumption is 270 kWh/t DS [12]; temperature driving force 60°C|★☆☆☆☆｜MVR energy figure for steam recompression|

|Ch32|p.750|数据|Availability & corrosion|flue-gas dryers availability typically 80%+; steam dryers 35-90% avg ~70%; condensate acidic and corrosive|★★☆☆☆｜Plant-reliability/corrosion reality for any dryer audit|
|Ch32|p.751|论断|Kinetics experience-based|Rather few papers deal with the kinetics of biofuel drying and most designs seem to be 'experience-based'|★★☆☆☆｜Honest admission; contrast with model-driven Ch30/Ch31|
|Ch32|p.751|论断|Intraparticle resistance|The intraparticle resistance is pronounced and, of course, controlled by the particle size|★★☆☆☆｜Particle-size controls internal resistance; analogy to fibre-bed permeability|
|Ch33|p.753|定义|Fibre aspect ratio|Fibers are regarded as very elongated particles. Staple fibers that are spun into yarns have length/diameter ratios greater than 10,000|★★★★★｜Defines the elongated polymeric particle that underlies fluff-pulp and nonwoven behaviour|
|Ch33|p.754|论断|Wood = lignin-bonded fibre composite|Wood consists of a large number of fibers... bonded together by lignin to form a solid and rigid structure... walls composed mainly of cellulose and its associated polysaccharides|★★★★★｜Anisotropic semicrystalline polymer composite; basis of pulp vs solid-wood behaviour|
|Ch33|p.754|数据|Anisotropic swelling factor|dimensional change is much smaller along the fiber length compared with the changes across the fiber by a factor of 50 to 100|★★★★★｜Predicts in-plane vs through-thickness dimensional change of cellulose webs|
|Ch33|p.754|论断|Pulping delignifies|Pulping, by chemical or thermomechanical means, delignifies the structure, releasing individual fibers|★★★★★｜Delignification = removing Tg-bonded matrix; creates loose swellable fluff|
|Ch33|p.755|数据|Table 33.1 sorption values|Woodpulp 0.034/0.062/0.25; cotton 0.0305/0.0565/0.23; viscose 0.0555/0.101/0.46 at RH 0.2/0.5/1.0 kg/kg|★★★★★｜Quantitative hygroscopicity ranking for fibre selection and SAP sizing|
|Ch33|p.755|论断|Monomolecular adsorption at low RH|At low relative humidities (0<p/p0<0.35), water is adsorbed monomolecularly by many natural fibers|★★★★☆｜First bound-water layer; governs low-MC equilibrium|
|Ch33|p.755|公式|BET sorption isotherm|X_e = X_1 C w / [(1-w)(1-w+Cw)] (Eq 33.2); Cassie: water sorbed as strongly localized or liquid-like|★★★★★｜Canonical bound-water isotherm for cellulose; underpins equilibrium-MC models|
|Ch33|p.755|论断|Fibre saturation point / capillary break|at relative humidities above 0.98, the lumens and pits... begin to fill by capillary condensation, causing a sharp upward break in the sorption curve|★★★★★｜Defines bound/free water boundary (FSP) — the master split for pressing vs drying|
|Ch33|p.756|数据|Heat of sorption vs vaporization|differential heat of sorption is about half that of vaporization, falling to about one quarter... when a complete monolayer is formed at about 4 to 5% moisture content|★★★★★｜Energetics of bound water; quantifies why bound water resists pressing|
|Ch33|p.756|论断|Swelling/shrinkage & permanent set|Water imbibed by the cell walls of fibers causes swelling and moisture loss causes shrinkage... creep strain that may not fully recover... known as permanent set|★★★★★｜Permanent set = macroscopic hornification analogue; irreversible drying damage|
|Ch33|p.756|论断|Water plasticizes fibres|With hydrophilic materials, moisture is found to reduce stiffness and increase creep, possibly as a result of plasticization|★★★★★｜Water = polymer plasticizer lowering effective Tg; explains redry embrittlement|
|Ch33|p.756|论断|Anisotropic shrinkage mechanism|Wood fibers shrink anisotropically on drying... microfibrils in the cell wall restraint the cell wall matrix of lignin and hemicelluloses|★★★★★｜Oriented semicrystalline restraint; micro-mechanism of web dimensional change|
|Ch33|p.757|论断|Diffusion driven by chemical potential|movement of water through a single fiber... occurs at a rate that depends on the chemical potential gradient|★★★★☆｜Correct driving force for moisture transport in fibres|
|Ch33|p.757|论断|Sorptive (activated) diffusion at low MC|at low moisture contents, sorbed water may form strong bonds with a hydrophilic fiber, so that simple diffusion can no longer occur. The concept of sorptive diffusion has been introduced... kinetic energies greater than the activation energy of the moisture-fiber bonds|★★★★★｜Molecular basis of hornification; site-locking on each redry|
|Ch33|p.757|公式|Fick first law (dry-basis)|J = -(D) rho_f (∂X/∂x) (Eq 33.14); Eq 33.12 is a form of Fick's first law of diffusion|★★★★☆｜Standard transport equation for fibre-web drying models|
|Ch33|p.758|公式|Cylindrical Fick second law|∂c/∂t = (1/r) ∂/∂r (r D ∂c/∂r) (Eq 33.15)|★★★★☆｜Single-fibre diffusion geometry; needed for intrafibre transport|
|Ch33|p.759|数据|Single-fibre diffusivity & response|moisture diffusion coefficient within a single wool fiber is of the order 10^-11 m^2 s^-1... 98% complete within 2.5 s|★★★★☆｜Single fibre equilibrates fast; mass limitation is interfibre, not intrafibre|
|Ch33|p.759|公式|Henry linearized bed response|X = X_0 + s c_v + v T (Eq 33.30); X_0 datum moisture content|★★★★☆｜Couples MC to air vapour concentration and temperature in a bed|
|Ch33|p.760|论断|Local hygrothermal equilibrium|a mass of fibers will react rapidly to its surrounding environment, and local hygrothermal equilibrium may often pertain|★★★★☆｜Justifies lumped/fast-equilibrium modelling of fibre masses|
|Ch33|p.761|数据|Bale penetration is slow|After 1 day, the moisture uptake is calculated to penetrate 150 mm into the fibrous mass, reaching 700 mm after 10 days and 1.1 m after 100 days|★★★★★｜Baled pulp equilibrates over weeks; storage conditioning time matters|
|Ch33|p.761|论断|High-density packing suppresses diffusion|When fiber is packed at high density, its equilibrium moisture content and the apparent moisture diffusion coefficient appear to fall... Moisture diffusion in the bale approaches that in a single fiber|★★★★★｜Bale density sets effective diffusivity; affects procurement/storage specs|
|Ch33|p.762|论断|Fibrous mass NOT capillary-porous|a mass of loose fibers do not constitute a simple capillary-porous body... mechanisms that give rise to a constant-rate period in a porous network do not arise in a touching fibrous assembly|★★★★★｜Corrects a common modelling error for nonwovens/airlaid webs|
|Ch33|p.762|论断|Three moisture-migration modes|moisture migration... (a) liquid diffusion along fibers; (b) capillarity and gravity within interfiber spaces; (c) vapor diffusion due to vapor-pressure variations|★★★★★｜Complete taxonomy of transport in a fibre web|
|Ch33|p.763|数据|Characteristic-curve exponent n|n = 0.6 (loose wool, Keey & Wu); n = 2/3 (thin balsa slats, Peck et al.); internal resistance negligible <6 mm thickness|★★★★☆｜Empirical kinetics for thin-web drying design|
|Ch33|p.763|论断|Intra- vs interfibre water in paper|Dry paper fiber consists of about 100 lamellae, but is not porous. Water can diffuse into the fiber... causing swelling across the fiber but not along it. Completely wet paper consists of moisture between fibers and bound water within the solid matrix|★★★★★｜Canonical intrafibre/interfibre water split for cellulose webs|

|Ch33|p.767|论断|Three stages of through-circulation|1. all MC above critical; 2. part below critical near band; 3. all below critical, profile differences diminish|★★★★☆｜Staged drying profile for band dryers|
|Ch33|p.768|论断|Airflow reversal evens moisture|by periodically changing the airflow direction along the band, the streamwise variation in moisture content can be restricted|★★★★☆｜Process lever to reduce over-drying of nonuniform webs|
|Ch33|p.769|论断|Impingement jet spacing|optimum clearance of circular air-jet nozzles is 5 times the nozzle diameter... optimum pitch/distance ratio 0.7 (Martin)|★★★★☆｜Design rule for impingement dryers on heavy fabrics|
|Ch33|p.770|数据|Paper machine water removal|water-to-fiber ratio about 6:1 at wire; press section removes about 70% of the water; sheet emerges at about 8% moisture content|★★★★★｜70% by pressing not heat — dewatering-first discipline|
|Ch33|p.771|数据|Multicylinder heat split|about two thirds of the heat demand... used directly in evaporation, while about 22% is lost from the cylinders... About 6% lost from the web by convection|★★★★☆｜Energy-loss breakdown for cylinder dryers|
|Ch33|p.773|论断|Lignin Tg bonding in press drying|thermal softening of lignin and cellulose... temperatures 25 to 50°C above the glass-transition point of lignin... bonding can take place... temperature in the order of 150°C or above in the wet web|★★★★★｜PoP anchor: lignin Tg as bonding switch; sets <150°C limit for fluffy fluff|
|Ch33|p.773|论断|Superheated-steam thermal damage|thermal damage to wool fibers in the presence of steam increased with temperature, exposure time, and the pH... Presence of water in the material enhanced the degradation|★★★★☆｜Water accelerates high-T degradation; steam-dry caution|
|Ch33|p.774|数据|Inversion temperature|evaporation into pure superheated vapor higher than into dry air above inversion; experimental 160 to 230°C; point inversion 155°C (Ti0,1) and 300°C (Ti1,1), common 198.68°C|★★★★☆｜Threshold where steam drying outpaces air drying|
|Ch34|p.779|定义|Textile includes nonwovens|textile is defined as any product made from fibers. Thus textiles refer not only to woven fabrics but also to nonwoven fabrics, knitted fabrics, tufted fabrics|★★★★★｜Chapter directly covers hygiene nonwoven substrates|
|Ch34|p.779|数据|Drying energy share|Approximately 25% of the energy used in wet processing is consumed in drying|★★★★★｜Drying is a major cost/bottleneck in substrate making|
|Ch34|p.779|论断|Mechanical << thermal|Mechanical dewatering is generally much less expensive than thermal drying. Thus, as much water as possible is usually removed mechanically|★★★★★｜Justifies maximizing wet-pickup reduction before dryer|

|Ch34|p.781|论断|Predry to ~30%|fabrics that are heat set ... predried ... to a moisture regain of approximately 30% ... tenter frame that fixes the width ... dimensional stability|★★★★★｜Heat-set fixes dimensions; relevant to substrate stability|

|Ch34|p.786|论断|Through-air principle|through-air drying greatly increases the contact surface area between the hot air and the wet surface, it provides high overall drying rate|★★★★★｜Through-air = high rate; core for nonwoven drying|
|Ch34|p.786|论断|Nonwovens use drum through-air|Suction or perforated drum dryers are often used to dry fabrics, particularly nonwovens, and sometimes unbacked tufted carpets|★★★★★｜Directly the hygiene nonwoven dryer type|
|Ch34|p.786|论断|Permeability gate|if air permeability of the material is too low, the electrical energy for driving the fan ... would be too high. In this case, impingement drying is often used instead|★★★★★｜Air permeability decides through-air vs impingement|
|Ch34|p.787|论断|Impingement use|impingement drying is often used to dry textiles with low air permeability, for example, latex compounds used to back tufted carpets ... thick fabrics and fabrics with backings|★★★★☆｜Impingement for low-perm/backed structures|
|Ch34|p.787|论断|Circulating-air loop dryers|Drying rates are usually low, and residence times ... loop dryers ... usually in the order of 40 to 45 min|★★★★☆｜Slow convection option; long residence|
|Ch34|p.788|论断|IR predry only|IR ovens are typically used to predry materials ... finally dried in tenter-frame convection ovens ... not normally used to accomplish final drying because ... danger of scorching or burning|★★★★★｜Scorch risk; IR not for final drying of thin webs|
|Ch34|p.788|公式|IR radiation|thermal radiation heat-transfer rate, q_rad, emitted by the IR emitter is equal to A epsilon sigma T^4|★★★★☆｜Stefan-Boltzmann; T^4 drives IR intensity|

|Ch34|p.788|论断|IR penetration limit|even at short wavelengths, the maximum depth is typically no greater than about 1.5 mm. Thus, IR drying is generally used for thin materials such as fabrics|★★★★☆｜IR surface-only; thin-web method|

|Ch35|p.791|数据|Consistency ladder|initial consistencies of 0.2 to 1.0% ... 18 to 23% after screen ... 33 to 55% before dryer ... 6 to 9% moisture content final|★★★★★｜Defines where pressing ends and thermal drying begins|

|Ch35|p.796|论断|Bleed practice poor|recent studies have showed that this practice (bleeds) is very poor and unnecessary|★★★★☆｜Modern steam systems matter for supplier efficiency|

|Ch35|p.803|论断|Through-air > Yankee|very high drying rates (which exceed those on Yankee cylinders with high-velocity hoods) ... even at fairly low temperatures|★★★★★｜Through-air delivers bulk + rate; wipe-substrate method|
|Ch35|p.803|论断|Not for newsprint|It is not recommended for newsprint, especially at the wet end, where the permeability is relatively low|★★★★☆｜Permeability governs method choice|
|Ch35|p.804|论断|Tension-free = bulk/stretch|drying under zero or moderate tension in the shrinkage range gives higher stretch and tensile energy absorption ... modulus of elasticity and stiffness decreases|★★★★★｜Spec lever for bulk vs modulus of wipe/substrate|
|Ch35|p.805|公式|Radiant heat|q_r = sigma A_1 F_(1-2) (T_1^4 - T_2^4) (Eq 35.12)|★★★★☆｜Radiation drying; contact-free coating dry|

|Ch35|p.810|数据|Press drying rate|average drying rates about 10-fold at 177C and 20-fold at 288C compared with conventional drying of linerboard|★★★★★｜Quantifies bond-building intensity at 177-288C|

|Ch35|p.815|论断|Flash lower-bound cross-linking|The lower moisture bound is critical, as overheating below it can cause thermal cross-linking that makes reconstitution of the original fiber difficult. Pulp temperature is maintained below 70C in both stages|★★★★★｜HORNIFICATION CLAUSE: governs defibration energy, knots, pad integrity, rewet|
|Ch35|p.815|数据|Flash two-stage|dries pulp from approximately 60 to 12% water in a two-stage dryer ... air inlet temperatures of 400 and 170C|★★★★★｜Stage temperatures to interrogate in supplier audit|
|Ch35|p.815|数据|Steam dryer cost|first commercial installation ... CTMP line ... 150 t/day showed a 30% reduction in overall drying costs per ton of market pulp compared with an equivalent-size flash dryer|★★★★★｜Negotiable cost lever if quality holds|
|Ch35|p.815|论断|Steam dryer advantages|Lower power consumption, very short drying times, easy control, no risk for fire in steam atmosphere, and minor or no effect on pulp quality|★★★★☆｜Quality-claim to verify on received pulp|

|Ch36|p.822|论断|Wood highly variable|wood is a variable material that is extremely difficult to characterize precisely. Hence wood processing, including drying, is very difficult to optimize|★★★★★｜Feedstock variability propagates into pulp uniformity|

|Ch36|p.825|论断|Lignin cementing agent|Lignin is often regarded as the cementing agent that binds individual cells together and is insoluble in most solvents|★★★★★｜Amorphous lignin matrix; PoP crossref (thermoplastic network)|

|Ch36|p.828|定义|FSP|FSP of the wood may be defined as the equilibrium moisture content (EMC) in an environment of 99% relative humidity. This yields a value of 30 to 32% for most commercial species|★★★★★｜Master free/bound water split; Ch33/Ch35 anchor|
|Ch36|p.828|数据|FSP vs temperature|FSP falls from about 31% at 25C to 23% at 100C (Sitka spruce)|★★★★☆｜FSP temperature dependence|
|Ch36|p.829|论断|Sorption hysteresis|ratio of values normally ranges between 0.75 and 0.88|★★★★☆｜Desorption>adsorption; equilibrium-MC caveat|

|Ch36|p.830|论断|Shrinkage mechanism|when sorbed water is removed from the cell wall, new hydrogen bonds form between the hydroxyl groups ... reduce the distance between the chains|★★★★★｜Molecular origin of hornification/shrinkage|
|Ch36|p.830|论断|Longitudinal shrink negligible|longitudinal shrinkage is usually negligible ... transverse directions ... close to a straight line|★★★★★｜Anisotropic shrinkage = oriented composite|

|Ch36|p.832|数据|Permeability anisotropy|longitudinal permeability can be 1000 times greater than the transverse ... more than a million-fold for hardwoods|★★★★★｜Fluid egress overwhelmingly longitudinal|
|Ch36|p.833|论断|Pit aspiration|It is impossible to avoid pit aspiration when drying softwoods under normal conditions|★★★★☆｜Locks in low permeability on drying|

|Ch36|p.837|公式|Bound-water diffusivity|D_b = 4.3e-10 exp(12.82 - 10.90 X_b - ...) [equation garbled in source]; D_b increases with bound-water content|★★★★★｜Falling-rate below FSP is bound-water diffusion limited|

|Ch36|p.841|论断|High-T overpressure|temperature of the product is above the boiling point of water ... an overpressure exists within the material|★★★★☆｜Internal vaporization accelerates drying|
|Ch36|p.843|数据|Sapwood vs heartwood|heartwood permeability so low ... maximum pressure is higher for heartwood than for sapwood|★★★★☆｜Divergent drying kinetics by zone|
|Ch36|p.845|论断|Shrinkage drives stress|Shrinkage is the 'driving' force for drying stress, i.e., without shrinkage, no drying stresses would develop|★★★★★｜Solid-wood analogue of permanent set|
|Ch36|p.846|论断|Stress reversal / case-hardening|This phenomenon is known as stress reversal or case-hardening|★★★★☆｜Residual stress from drying history|
|Ch36|p.847|论断|Memory effect|memory effect of wood depends not only on the temperature and moisture content values but also on their variations in time|★★★★☆｜Mechanical hysteresis of drying state|
|Ch36|p.852|公式|Mechanosorptive creep|strain rate depends linearly on both the stress field and the time derivative of the moisture content (Eq 36.21)|★★★｜Moisture-change-driven creep|
|Ch36|p.854|论断|Diffusion slows at low MC|diffusion becomes very slow when the bound-water content decreases toward zero|★★★★★｜Rate limit approaching oven-dry|
|Ch36|p.855|论断|Rule 1 high RH|Rule 1: high relative humidity. To ensure a low moisture content gradient, reduce the drying potential (wet-bulb depression)|★★★★☆｜Fast-good drying rule set|
|Ch36|p.855|论断|Rule 2 high temperature|Rule 2: high temperature. accelerates the internal moisture transfer and activates the viscoelastic creep|★★★★☆｜High T aids transfer + creep relief|
|Ch36|p.857|论断|Green MC softwood|green moisture content in softwoods lies in the range of 150 to 200% in sapwood and in the range of 30 to 80% in heartwood|★★★★★｜Repeats Table 36.3; feedstock water burden|
|Ch36|p.858|论断|Segregate heart/sap|it may be important to segregate heartwood from sapwood because of the large differences in moisture saturation and drying behavior|★★★★☆｜Sort feedstock by zone for uniform drying|

|Ch39|p.914|论断|Spray at wet-bulb for labile|spray drying can also be applied to thermolabile substances provided the sprayed material is highly dispersed and the drying agent has a high temperature (the drying process takes place immediately at a wet bulb temperature). Thus, the product quality is not deteriorated|★★★★★｜High-inlet-air + dispersion keeps particle at wet-bulb = safe for actives|
|Ch39|p.914|论断|Four recommended solutions|1. Low-intense conditions; 2. Vacuum or freeze drying; 3. Application of multistage systems; 4. Special techniques based on the decrease of initial material moisture content|★★★★☆｜Process-design menu for preserving bioactivity|
|Ch39|p.914|论断|Multistage dryer|The first stage is a spray dryer in which material is predried... The second and third stages are fluidized or vibrofluidized bed dryers in which slow drying or cooling of the product takes place|★★★★☆｜Two/three-stage template protects activity like Ch40/Ch41|
|Ch39|p.915|论断|Spray dryer disadvantages|scaling up of dryers under mild thermal conditions (at 150 to 200 C), expensive devices for liquid dispersion and dust removal... deposition of a product on chamber walls|★★★｜Spray-dry limitations for thermolabile actives|
|Ch39|p.917|论断|Freeze/vacuum advantages|Moisture is removed at low temperature, which in fact excludes thermal inactivation of the product. The material structure is maintained (particles and cells are not damaged)|★★★★★｜Low-T route that preserves live cells/enzymes|
|Ch39|p.917|论断|Freeze disadvantages|The main disadvantages of freeze drying include long process duration, high energy consumption, and complicated equipment|★★★｜Trade-off cost of maximal activity preservation|
|Ch39|p.917|论断|Self-freezing vitrification|at ultra-swift cooling (e.g., at the rate of 10,000 C/min)... amorphous ice is formed (transformation into a vitreous form, termed vitrification), which does not cause mechanical damage of cells|★★★★☆｜Rapid freezing avoids crystal damage; glass-state protection|
|Ch39|p.918|论断|Protective substances|The presence of protective substances in dried material, such as glycerin and sugars (fructose, saccharose, lactose...)... protect microorganisms against too high salt concentration|★★★★★｜Cryoprotectant formulation lever for active survival|
|Ch39|p.918|论断|Glycerin forms vitreous structures|In the temperature range from 20 to 80 C glycerin controls the crystallization process and makes it reversible... contributes to the formation of vitreous structures|★★★★☆｜Glycerin as glass-former/stabilizer in frozen matrix|
|Ch39|p.918|数据|Freeze T below -40C|in most substances the freeze-drying temperature should be lower than -40 C because at this temperature a significant part of moisture remains unfrozen|★★★★☆｜Lower-bound freeze temperature for complete sublimation|

The carrier as a sorbent takes over part of moisture from biomass and thus the amount of water to be evaporated is smaller|★★★★☆｜Carrier doubles as protectant and energy saver|
|Ch39|p.921|图表|Fig 39.22 sorbent K|Relative absorptivity K of some sorbents: ZeoliteCaA; activated carbon; wheat bran; maize meal; peat; kaolin; potato flour; silica gel|★★★｜Sorbent ranking to select a food-grade carrier|
|Ch39|p.922|论断|Encapsulation for stable storage|encapsulation of valuable substances and next drying of these systems so that they could be stored in a stable state... most often applied in cosmetic, pharmaceutical, and food industries|★★★★☆｜Microencapsulation stabilizes actives for wipes|

|Ch40|p.930|定义|Coating components|Coatings, inks, and adhesives contain more than one component: (1) a binder... (2) a variety of additives... (3) surface particles... (4) a liquid solvent to dissolve or suspend all particles|★★★★★｜Binder+additive+solvent structure governs migration and defect behavior|
|Ch40|p.930|论断|Solvent needs closed cycle|Organic solvents require use of a closed-cycle operation and special care in design and operation owing to the potential fire|★★★★☆｜Solvent coatings (some adhesive/print lines) need closed-cycle safety|
|Ch40|p.930|论断|100% solids cures chemically|A 100% solids coating is a liquid that does not contain any solvent or water. It changes into a solid through a chemical action (i.e., exposure... curing of the coating)|★★★★★｜UV/EB lotion/coating route needs no evaporative dryer|
|Ch40|p.931|论断|Heat/mass at proper time|the proper quantity of heat and mass transfer has to be applied to the coating at the proper time in the drying curve|★★★★★｜Drying schedule (not just total energy) sets final wipe/film quality|
|Ch40|p.931|论断|Three drying-rate periods|(1) the adjustment stage or the cooling-down period... A-B; (2) the constant drying rate period... B-C; and (3) the falling drying rate period... C-D|★★★★★｜The stage framework for any coated-web dryer design|
|Ch40|p.931|数据|Latex longer constant-rate|the drying curve of water-based latex coatings has a longer constant drying rate than solvent-based coatings. Paper... constant drying rate period even longer than latex coatings|★★★★★｜Water/lotion (latex-like) coatings enjoy a longer safe constant-rate window|

|Ch40|p.931|论断|Binder migration -> mottle|most coated papers are intended for printing, and binder migration causing printing mottles should be eliminated or minimized during drying operation|★★★★★｜Mottle mechanism = binder migration; uniform active-distribution issue for wipes|

|Ch40|p.933|数据|Latex permits 35 kg/m2h|synthetic binders (e.g., latex) permit drying rates of up to 35 kg/m2 h. Little migration occurs once the coating is about 75% dryness|★★★★★｜Quantified rate ceiling and the 75%-dryness migration guardrail|
|Ch40|p.933|论断|Impingement accelerates migration|the high-velocity air impinging directly on the coating surface tends to dry the surface coating and accelerates the binder migration in the coating|★★★★★｜Impingement over-dries surface and worsens migration; use with care on wipes|
|Ch40|p.933|论断|Railroad tracking vs wet-bulb|Rail road tracking will take place when the dryer temperature exceeds the wet bulb temperature of the constant drying rate period|★★★★★｜Named defect + explicit temperature ceiling (wet-bulb) for coated films|

|Ch40|p.940|定义|IR wavelength regions|IR wavelength falls between the wavelengths of 0.76 and 100 mm, which can be divided into three regions: (1) short-wave IR (0.76 to 2 mm); (2) medium-wave IR (2.1 to 4 mm); and (3) long-wave IR (4.1 to 100 mm)|★★★★☆｜IR band selection for coating preheat|

|Ch40|p.945|数据|Inert gas O2 <5%|In actual operation, oxygen levels are monitored and controlled within the dryer to maintain <5% oxygen|★★★★★｜Inert-gas dryer O2 control enables safe high-solvent recirculation|

|Ch41|p.957|论断|FBD advantages|(1) even flow permits continuous operation; (2) ability to handle polydisperse solids; (3) low aeration rates/lower pressure drops; (4) high heat transfer to immersed panels; (5) rapid mixing causes nearly isothermal conditions|★★★★★｜FBD gives isothermal, uniform-temperature drying = low thermal-crosslink risk|

|Ch41|p.957|数据|Indirect FBD heat-sensitive list|Indirect fluid beds have already proved efficient in drying very heat-sensitive polymers with large constant-rate drying periods, as in drying PVC, polyethylene, acrylonitrile-butadiene-styrene (ABS) copolymers, and polycarbonates (PC)|★★★★★｜Lists the hygiene-relevant heat-sensitive polymer families|

|Ch41|p.960|论断|Continuator for entrapped volatiles|The Continuator is used primarily for removing tightly entrapped volatiles and for process applications requiring a long residence time|★★★★★｜Long-residence device for tightly-entrapped volatiles = SAP residual-monomer strip|

|Ch41|p.964|论断|Rapid evaporation protects quality|Rapid evaporation maintains a low temperature of the spray droplets so that high dry gas temperature can be applied without affecting polymer quality|★★★★★｜Fast surface evaporation avoids skinning/case-hardening and thermal damage|

|Ch41|p.965|数据|Rotary dryer legacy spec|Traditionally, a rotary dryer system was applied to achieve a final moisture content of 0.2%. Rotary dryers for the purpose are typically 1 to 2 m diameter and 15 to 30 m long, rotating at 4 to 8 rpm|★★★｜Legacy rotary spec; shows evolution to two-stage FBD|
|Ch41|p.965|数据|Flash+FBD 30 min bound|some surface and all of the bound moisture are removed in the fluid-bed stage by holding the product at suitable drying temperatures for about 30 min|★★★★☆｜30 min bound-moisture residence; analog to SAP finishing dwell|

|Ch41|p.971|数据|PET 0.15% storage -> 0.005% db|PET in normal storage conditions contains about 0.15% moisture (db). It must be dried to a level of 0.005% (db) or better for processing|★★★★★｜PET internal-moisture spec; below this hydrolysis is controlled|
|Ch41|p.971|论断|No atmosphere exposure PET|Although PET is not difficult to dry because of the high temperature that can be used, it can have absolutely no exposure to atmosphere between drying and processing operations|★★★★★｜Sealed transfer needed; moisture regain ruins spinnability|
